### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/TCP4D_A_Unified_Tool-Center-Point_Tracking_and_Generation_Model_for_Robot_Manipulation|TCP4D: A Unified Tool-Center-Point Tracking and Generation Model for Robot Manipulation]]

![[assets/2611.23143_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.23143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23143)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23143
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/TCP4D_A_Unified_Tool-Center-Point_Tracking_and_Generation_Model_for_Robot_Manipulation|TCP4D: A Unified Tool-Center-Point Tracking and Generation Model for Robot Manipulation]]
- **作者**: Haosong Peng, Xiangyang Liu, Zhengshen Zhang, Yunfeng Fan, Tianyu Qi, Xin Xia, Yufeng Zhan, Wenchao Xu
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.6（加权：具身智能 1.5，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《TCP4D: A Unified Tool-Center-Point Tracking and Generation Model for Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot manipulation videos provide a rich visual experience, yet recovering robot action and gripper-state annotations from RGB observations still remains challenging. Using the embodiment-agnostic tool-center-point (TCP) pose as the action space, we present TCP4D, a unified model in which a shared 4D scene representation both tracks observed TCP motion and generates future TCP trajectories. Given a monocular video and initial 2D TCP queries, the tracker directly recovers metric TCP poses in SE(3) and gripper states alongside scene geometry and camera parameters. The generator samples local features from the same 4D representation along the recovered TCP tracks, then combines them with robot observations and language instructions to predict future TCP states. Our new cross-embodiment augmentation pipeline converts existing RGB videos into demonstrations for alternative embodiments by retargeting the recovered motion and synthesizing the corresponding observations, without recorded source-robot states. Experiments show that the tracker achieves absolute trajectory errors of 7.60 and 16.07 mm on DROID and RoboLab and adapts to a new embodiment with only ten demonstrations. The generator achieves 69.19% mean success on RoboTwin and outperforms well-established policy models on two real-world tasks, while joint training on source and augmented demonstrations improves target-robot success by 41.5% over augmented-only training.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/CADENCE_Learning_Acyclic_Gaits_for_Model-Based_Quadruped_Locomotion|CADENCE: Learning Acyclic Gaits for Model-Based Quadruped Locomotion]]

![[assets/2611.23102_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.23102](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23102)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23102
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/CADENCE_Learning_Acyclic_Gaits_for_Model-Based_Quadruped_Locomotion|CADENCE: Learning Acyclic Gaits for Model-Based Quadruped Locomotion]]
- **作者**: Owen Sullivan, Sourav Selvaraj, Mahdi Agheli
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 强化学习
- **相关性评分**: 3.7（加权：具身智能 2.4，强化学习 0.2，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《CADENCE: Learning Acyclic Gaits for Model-Based Quadruped Locomotion》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ContactNet, GaitNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Model-based quadruped controllers usually step on a cyclic gait, which lifts the legs in a fixed order on a fixed period, wherever the safe footholds are. We present CADENCE, a learned step planner with a small action space. Every 40 ms, it picks one of at most 65 discrete choices: no step, or one leg's step to a safe foothold. It also predicts a swing time. We train CADENCE by reinforcement learning on top of a convex model predictive controller (MPC). Nothing prescribes a gait: there is no gait schedule, and the reward has no gait term, only a cost per step. The one structural constraint is that at most two legs may swing at once. The policy we analyzed developed an acyclic gait, with no fixed period or stepping order, yet it still paired diagonal legs. An inverse footstep model (IFM), fitted on a controller's logged steps, turns each foothold into a command. With only the IFM refitted, on fewer than 20,000 steps, CADENCE ran frozen on OCS2's nonlinear MPC. In simulated trials on a range of terrains (26% to 49% voids) and speeds (0.1 m/s and 0.2 m/s), it succeeded in 85.1% (mean of five seeds), against 67.8% for OCS2-Trot, 65.5% for OCS2-Single, and 39.6% in a static walk ablation of CADENCE. At 49% voids, it succeeded in 72.9%, against 43.0% for OCS2-Trot.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/PAIR_Bringing_Household_Robots_into_the_Smart_Home|PAIR: Bringing Household Robots into the Smart Home]]

![[assets/2611.23070_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.23070](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23070)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23070
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/PAIR_Bringing_Household_Robots_into_the_Smart_Home|PAIR: Bringing Household Robots into the Smart Home]]
- **作者**: Junseong Pyo, Hyeongjin Kim, Jongwook Han, Seungwon Kook, Sungjib Lim, Yohan Jo
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.0（加权：具身智能 1.5，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《PAIR: Bringing Household Robots into the Smart Home》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Household robots often need to operate appliances, and smart appliances offer both control panels and device APIs. Existing embodied AI simulation environments, however, model appliances solely as physical objects. We introduce PAIR, a smart-home simulation environment grounded in the Matter protocol~\footnote{https://csa-iot.org/all-solutions/matter/}, the industry standard for smart-home devices. PAIR provides 84 smart-device assets whose functional control panels and APIs act on a shared device state, with ambient conditions that evolve in response to both. From a survey of 1,443 requests, we construct a benchmark of 40 tasks that all require object manipulation yet remain solvable without API access, with 340 human demonstrations for policy training. Whether run alone, with a high-level planner, or with an API-enabled planner, two fine-tuned VLA policies complete none of the 120 evaluation episodes. Robots rarely get far enough to use the API: even with oracle plans and API access, over 90\% of failed rollouts stall at navigating, grasping, or operating a door. With a perfect oracle in place of the robot's body, the best planner still gets through only 57.3\% of its plan. When choosing between pressing a button and calling an API, several planners go by whether they can read the panel, not by API latency.\footnote{We will release our code and data upon publication.}

</details>

- **作者**: Jiarui Guo, Saining Zhang, Jiaying Wan, Jiawei Weng, Junhao Chen, Rui Zhang, Yuanbo Lyu, Zhiheng Lee, Peishuo Li, Jingrun Du, Weiqing Xiao, Weiyi Lin...
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《RFW-S: Everyday Deformable Objects with Independent Simulation Verification》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ClothesNet, DaXBench, DefGraspSim, Real2Sim, SoftGym。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large-scale robotic simulation requires diverse deformable objects with executable physical properties and realistic interaction behaviors. However, existing 3D asset collections primarily emphasize visual diversity, while scalable physical construction and verification remain challenging. We introduce RFW-S, a large-scale dataset of 83,365 simulation-verified assets spanning 485 everyday categories across five structural families: soft solids, cloth sheets, shells, garments, and bags. We develop an automated pipeline integrating reference-guided 3D generation, structure-aware physical modeling, and LLM-assisted parameter estimation and calibration. To ensure physical reliability, we introduce an independent simulation verification framework that evaluates object behaviors under interactions distinct from those used for parameter calibration. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of independent verification and characterize deformable responses under different physical configurations. We further evaluate robotic grasping in terms of both task success and object integrity, and demonstrate the physical realism of our simulated objects through sim-to-real replay of fragile-object manipulation. RFW-S provides a scalable foundation for physically grounded simulation and embodied robotic learning.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Physics-Informed_Tactile-based_Shape_Estimation_of_Deformable_Linear_Objects|Physics-Informed Tactile-based Shape Estimation of Deformable Linear Objects]]

![[assets/2611.22990_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22990](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22990)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22990
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Physics-Informed_Tactile-based_Shape_Estimation_of_Deformable_Linear_Objects|Physics-Informed Tactile-based Shape Estimation of Deformable Linear Objects]]
- **作者**: Rotem Atari, Stav Ashur, Avishai Sintov
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 1.1（加权：具身智能 0.6，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《Physics-Informed Tactile-based Shape Estimation of Deformable Linear Objects》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Accurate shape estimation of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) is a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation, often hindered by visual occlusions and the high-dimensional configuration space of flexible materials. While Force/Torque (F/T) and vision-centric methods have been proposed, they frequently struggle with grasp-site ambiguities and environmental interference. In this letter, we investigate the hypothesis that global shape estimation can be achieved through tactile sensing and proprioception, without any visual perception. We contend that dense, high-resolution tactile imprints capture critical boundary conditions and contact gradients that are sufficient to resolve the high-dimensional configuration of the DLO. To prove this, we introduce a Physics-Informed Vision Transformer (ViT) framework that maps tactile images to a 3D vertex chain. By leveraging self-attention mechanisms and a physics-weighted loss function, our model enforces structural priors to ensure that the predicted geometry adheres to the object's physical manifold. Experimental results demonstrate that our tactile-based approach achieves superior reconstruction accuracy across Euclidean and Chamfer metrics. Furthermore, we demonstrate the efficiency of this modality, achieving model transfer to novel DLO materials and geometries. Our findings suggest that tactile feedback provides a robust and computationally efficient alternative for DLO state estimation in visually degraded or occluded environments.

</details>

![[assets/2611.22880_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22880)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22880
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Learning_Agent-Centric_Vision-Language-Action_World_Models_for_Autonomous_Driving|Learning Agent-Centric Vision-Language-Action World Models for Autonomous Driving]]
- **作者**: Guoqing Wang, Pin Tang, Xiangxuan Ren, Chao Ma
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 世界模型, 大模型, 强化学习
- **相关性评分**: 2.9（加权：具身智能 1.5，大模型 0.4，强化学习 0.2，世界模型 0.8）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Agent, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning Agent-Centric Vision-Language-Action World Models for Autonomous Driving》归入 具身智能、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AgentWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recent progress in integrating vision-language-action (VLA) models with world modeling has significantly advanced end-to-end autonomous driving by allowing systems to reason about imagined futures instead of merely reacting to current observations. However, most world VLA models generate future frames at the pixel level with plenty of dense visual tokens. Although such generated futures can be visually plausible, they preserve many driving-irrelevant appearance details, i.e., only a small portion of the tokens directly encode critical planning-relevant factors, such as agent geometry, motion dynamics, and interactions. This introduces a significant performance bottleneck. An important question thus arises: what world state should a world VLA model reason over to support planning beyond pixel-level prediction? We argue that planning-oriented world modeling should focus on a compact and structured agent-centric latent state that captures the interactions between objects in the driving scene over time. As such, we introduce AgentWorld, an agent-centric VLA world model that uses discrete agent tokens jointly for reasoning and trajectory planning. Each token is dynamically grounded to a surrounding agent associated with its object's state, such as semantic identity, 3D geometry, motion, and interaction context. AgentWorld learns these tokens through three key processes: explicit geometric grounding, reasoning-oriented supervised fine-tuning, and agent-oriented reinforcement learning. Extensive open-loop and closed-loop experiments under diverse driving scenarios show that AgentWorld enhances planning capabilities, fully exploits visual evidence and agent dynamics, and yields interpretable agent-centric world states.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Outcome-Balanced PPO and Policy-Induced Flow Rectification for Flow-Based VLA Policies》归入 具身智能、强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning (RL) enables post-training of flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies by optimizing expected task return through environment interaction. However, this optimization raises two challenges: policy updates can alter the velocity-field geometry underlying the score approximation used for stochastic transitions, and sparse multi-task rollouts can yield uneven learning signals across tasks and advantage signs. We introduce an RL framework that combines Outcome-Balanced Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with policy-induced flow rectification. Outcome-Balanced PPO reweights positive advantages using task success-rate estimates and rescales positive and negative advantages while preserving their signs. Policy-induced rectification periodically fits a linear-bridge flow to ODE endpoint pairs generated by the current policy, refreshing the representation used in subsequent stochastic optimization. We evaluate the framework on simulated manipulation benchmarks and additionally study a component combination in a Real2Sim2Real task, examining task performance, optimization behavior, and flow consistency. The proposed framework improves success rates over the evaluated baselines across the simulated benchmarks.

</details>

《Zero-Shot Compositional Generalization for Embodied Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, UniVLA, X-VLA, XL-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot demonstrations are collected on different embodiments, leaving many robot-skill combinations unobserved. Pooling these data shares visual knowledge, while joint labels remain tied to the demonstrating kinematic chain. We propose a geometry-supervised training framework for zero-shot composition of familiar robots and skills. The central insight is that a demonstrated command provides a tool-space target for every robot that can realize it. Calibrated forward kinematics supplies this supervision to foreign native action interfaces without their joint labels for that skill. Native reconstruction retains demonstrated configurations, and positive reachability checks select targets for cross-robot supervision. A visual policy learns to predict shared actions and executes through the target robot's native decoder. During policy learning, one prediction receives geometric supervision through both source and foreign native realizations. Matched supervision controls, repeated policy training and evaluations across three backbones and two simulators demonstrate improved transfer to missing robot-task combinations.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Network-Exposed Configuration Attacks on LLM-Controlled Social Robots》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) in embodied robots create security dependencies beyond inference. We investigate a network-exposed configuration attack on the Pollen Robotics Reachy Mini Lite, allowing an unauthenticated attacker to replace personality settings and the cloud LLM's system prompt through a management interface, without adversarial conversational input. Eleven prompt-injection attempts across five categories were reported as resisted, whereas both configuration-layer attack paths produced observable behavioural changes on physical hardware. We demonstrate local and network-adjacent access, mapping the weakness to CWE-306 (Missing Authentication for a Critical Function) and CWE-1327 (Binding to an Unrestricted IP Address), with a reported CVSS v3.1 score of 6.5 (Medium). A configuration endpoint also accepts cross-origin requests without a CORS preflight (CWE-352; 6.5 Medium), while the persistent-memory sink stores content without enforcing its stated restrictions. A source review of 59 public repositories identifies missing authentication in 10 of 11 source-verifiable genuine applications, or 11 of 11 including a framework-default case. These findings indicate recurrence within the sampled repositories, not prevalence in commercial products. We characterise a deployment gap between developer-oriented network assumptions and consumer-facing embodied AI security: inference-layer refusal cannot prevent unauthorised changes to model instructions. Potential consequences include surveillance through onboard cameras and microphones and social engineering of nearby users. Proof-of-concept code, evidence and the survey are available at https://bit.ly/reachypi.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《RefCloth: Transferable Adversarial Tablecloths for Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, SpatialVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Closed-loop vision-language-action (VLA) policies rely continuously on multi-view cameras, yet existing visual attacks typically hijack sensor streams with 2D patches or alter target object meshes that require retraining when manipulation objects change. We introduce RefCloth, a framework that optimizes one persistent adversarial tablecloth on a frozen source policy and deploys it zero-shot across unseen tasks and target models without modifying task objects or cameras. Throughout manipulation, static third-person and moving wrist views observe this shared world-bound texture under evolving occlusions. To generalize across shifting surface views while bridging the gap between differentiable and native renderers, RefCloth combines bounded texture parameterization with shared surface UV transformations and a same-path clean reference that removes static rendering offsets at initialization. Across four LIBERO suites in native simulation, one frozen texture transfers from OpenVLA-OFT to $π_0$ and $π_{0.5}$, reducing success relative to an unoptimized cloth by 62.50 and 43.75 percentage points on average and far exceeding amplitude- and variation-matched random controls. Dual-view audits and target closed-loop camera interventions show that moving wrist exposure drives over twice the standalone drop of the main view with strong synergy, while ablations confirm the roles of UV diversification and path calibration.

</details>

- **作者**: Dohyeon Kim, Seungyong Han, Yeongtae Jung

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning Accurate End-Effector Trajectory Tracking with Inertial Feedback for a Quadruped Manipulator》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Accurate end-effector trajectory tracking on quadruped manipulators is challenging because uncertainties in actuators, foot-ground contact, and geometric errors are propagated through the leg-arm kinematic chain. Detecting such errors requires sensing beyond joint encoders that can capture the effects of these uncertainties on the robot's motion, since joint encoders only provide measurements at the actuated joints and cannot fully observe mechanical errors such as backlash, dimensional inaccuracies, or structural deformation. This work addresses these challenges using a reinforcement learning framework with end-effector (EE) inertial feedback, targeting local high-accuracy manipulation while maintaining a nominal body pose and support configuration without intentional body motion or foot repositioning. The learned policy uses EE inertial measurements to compensate for tracking errors induced by uncertainties throughout the leg-arm system that cannot be fully inferred from proprioception alone. Simulation and real-world experiments on continuous EE trajectory tracking show overall improvements relative to nominal and proprioception-only baselines. In real-world experiments, the proposed policy reduces the MoCap-based RMSE by 17.3% and 28.0% relative to the nominal baseline under the two in-distribution (ID) command conditions, respectively, and by an additional 12.6% and 15.9% relative to the proprioception-only baseline. Furthermore, the trajectory-wise RMSE between FK-reconstructed and motion-capture-measured end-effector positions averaged 12.61+-2.15mm (mean+-standard deviation) across 24 controller-trajectory combinations, revealing a substantial discrepancy between nominal kinematic reconstruction and measured end-effector motion.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《VSpark: Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, Spec-VLA, StarVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Our aim is to accelerate autoregressive vision-language-action models (VLAs), which generate robot actions one token at a time. Speculative decoding makes this possible: a lightweight draft model proposes several tokens at once, and the VLA verifies them in a single pass, accepting only those that match its own predictions, so decoding remains lossless. The speedup, however, is limited in robot control, where many different tokens encode nearly the same motion and a draft close to the correct action is still rejected. To address this, our approach, VSpark, is built upon three design choices: (i) rollouts collected with calibrated action noise, which expose the draft to a wider range of robot states; (ii) feature alignment, which trains the draft to match the VLA's hidden features rather than its output tokens alone; and (iii) continuation reuse, which keeps the predictions the VLA has already computed along rejected drafts as candidates for the next verification step, instead of discarding them. On four VLAs with different action tokenizers and four manipulation benchmarks, VSpark achieves 1.28x to 2.10x speedup while keeping the VLA's decoding lossless.

</details>

《InteractVLA: Turning VLAs into Interactive Tools for Agentic Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：InteractVLA, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Existing agentic manipulation systems treat pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies as opaque, language-only primitives: when a policy call fails under unfamiliar instructions or scene perturbations, the agent cannot steer its behavior and is forced into futile retries or costly manual takeover. We present InteractVLA, a framework that turns pretrained flow-based VLAs into geometrically steerable and inspectable tools through a bidirectional geometric interface with vision-language agents. In the forward direction, test-time geometric guidance translates an agent-specified end-effector target into input gradients through the action expert via a differentiable command-to-pose mapping, while soft-time target alignment treats arrival time as a latent execution index to match the target pose without distorting the policy's approach motion. In the reverse direction, action-informed agentic refinement exposes generated action chunks and calibrated camera reprojections back to the agent, allowing it to diagnose spatial discrepancies, revise targets for subsequent VLA calls, or locally adjust motion segments on top of the guided trajectory. Making the VLA controllable not only boosts online agentic tool use, but also unlocks effective recursive self-improvement (RSI), achieving state-of-the-art performance of 99.4\% on LIBERO-Plus, 48.62\% on RoboDojo, and 89.4\% on RoboCasa365. Project page: https://lingwupro.github.io/InteractVLA/.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Progress_Predicts_Policy|Progress Predicts Policy]]

![[assets/2611.22491_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22491](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22491)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22491
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Progress_Predicts_Policy|Progress Predicts Policy]]
- **作者**: Licheng Zhong, Gim Hee Lee

#### 研究背景与动机

《Progress Predicts Policy》归入 具身智能、机器人、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ProgressVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A task specifies how the world should change, while a policy specifies what the robot should do. We introduce Progress Predicts Policy (PPP), which learns a language-conditioned scalar field through the commands induced by its differential. We obtain action conditioning by composing a latent progress field with a shared world model and read its action gradient at a fixed reference command. Continuous commands scale this gradient by a learned positive gain, while discrete commands use finite differences of the same scalar. This yields a hybrid policy without a separate action head or iterative search. Fitting demonstration progress values alone does not ensure that action gradients align with expert commands. PPP trains the induced commands by imitation and uses double backpropagation for the continuous gradient readout to shape the field's local control geometry. Demonstration timing calibrates field values on observed states without additional annotation. The continuous readout remains learnable without progress labels but these timing-derived targets support progress monitoring during execution. Experiments in simulated and real robot manipulation show that PPP outperforms behavioral cloning under a shared architecture.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/VoxelSBFL_Voxel-Level_Spectrum-Based_Failure_Prediction_for_Vision-Language-Action_Models|VoxelSBFL: Voxel-Level Spectrum-Based Failure Prediction for Vision-Language-Action Models]]

![[assets/2611.22453_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Arusa Kanwal, Pablo Valle, Paola Roggero, Miren Illarramendi, Shaukat Ali, Aitor Arrieta
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO, cs.SE

#### 研究背景与动机

《VoxelSBFL: Voxel-Level Spectrum-Based Failure Prediction for Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models integrate visual perception, language understanding, and action generation to control robots. Testing these systems produces trajectories and task outcomes, but existing approaches provide limited guidance on using these recorded trajectories to assess subsequent executions or inform policy repair. We conjecture that, for a given policy and task, traversing certain workspace regions is associated with a higher likelihood of failure. To investigate this conjecture, we propose VoxelSBFL, a black-box adaptation of Spectrum-Based Fault Localization (SBFL) in which workspace voxels replace program elements as units of analysis. Recorded tool-center-point (TCP) trajectories provide binary voxel coverage, which is combined with execution outcomes to construct suspiciousness maps using established SBFL formulas. These maps identify failure-associated regions and score unseen trajectories without requiring internal model access. We evaluate 34 spectrum-based techniques across four VLA models using 96,000 Pick-Up trajectories in simulation and compare the leading techniques against eight action- and trajectory-based metrics. VoxelSBFL obtains an overall macro ROC-AUC of 0.704, significantly outperforming state-of-the art baselines. Sensitivity analyses show that performance varies with spatial granularity and training-set size. These findings support spatial spectra for VLA execution assessment and motivate future research on runtime monitoring, self-healing and targeted policy improvement.

</details>

- **作者**: Koki Inami, Takashi Sato, Hiroyuki Oka, Shota Takizawa, Yuya Takahashi, Amon Suzuki, Hayato Ono, Kohtaro Ihara, Yuki Noka, Shohei Matsugu
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《Autonomous Track Racing with a Commercially Available Humanoid: System Design and Real-World Deployment》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：BezierLaneNet, CLRerNet, GANet, Real-World, ResNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Learning-based methods now let humanoids run robustly, and they have begun to compete in athletic events. Yet few reports describe a complete autonomous humanoid running system in an actual race, where the robot must follow a lane at a new venue, pace itself within hardware limits, and finish without intervention. We report such a system deployed on the Unitree G1, a commercially available general-purpose humanoid, in the 100 m, 400 m, and 1500 m events of the World Humanoid Robot Games 2026. The system integrates vision-based lane perception, path tracking, and a running policy trained by reinforcement learning with a human motion prior. To address the challenges of humanoid running, we stabilize head-camera images against body motion and use IMU-assisted leg odometry to bridge flight phases. To manage motor heating, we penalize hip-pitch torque during training, add heat-dissipation plates, and apply temperature-based speed limiting with event-specific pacing. The robot placed 13th of 74 in the 100 m, 10th of 44 in the 400 m first round before falling in the semifinal, and seventh of 35 in the 1500 m. In this deployment, thermal margin set the pace in every event, and the semifinal fall followed a lane-detection error.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/CoBoVLA_Decoupling_Language_from_Action_with_Discrete_Codes_for_Robust_and_Interpretable_Robot_Manipulation|CoBoVLA: Decoupling Language from Action with Discrete Codes for Robust and Interpretable Robot Manipulation]]

![[assets/2611.22395_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22395)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22395
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/CoBoVLA_Decoupling_Language_from_Action_with_Discrete_Codes_for_Robust_and_Interpretable_Robot_Manipulation|CoBoVLA: Decoupling Language from Action with Discrete Codes for Robust and Interpretable Robot Manipulation]]
- **作者**: Mehul Arora, Dhruv Agarwal, Ambuj Agrawal
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.7（加权：具身智能 1.8，机器人 0.9）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Agent, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《CoBoVLA: Decoupling Language from Action with Discrete Codes for Robust and Interpretable Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CoBoVLA, CodeBookVLA, NanoVLA, OpenVLA, ParaVLA, RoVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve high success rates on the LIBERO benchmark but remain sensitive to paraphrasing. Existing VLAs ranging from 0.6B to 7.5B parameters experience a 22-52 point performance drop on LIBERO-Para while SmolVLA exhibits an even larger decline, from 83\% to 8\%. Because most VLAs jointly learn language understanding and action generation, it is difficult to determine whether a failure originates from misunderstanding an instruction or executing it incorrectly. To address these challenges, we introduce CoBoVLA, or CodeBookVLA, a 250M-parameter VLA that decouples language understanding, planning, and control through learned discrete interpretable codes. A text encoder maps the instructions to shared step codes, which a visual planner then converts into action code sequences executed by a language-independent actor. This design improves robustness to instruction paraphrasing while allowing failures to be traced to individual stages. It also enables efficient real-time control on resource-constrained hardware by encoding instructions only once and using compact discrete representations to keep each component lightweight. CoBoVLA achieves 91.4\% success on LIBERO-Goal, compared with 82.8\% for SmolVLA, under a matched protocol, with 17 ms per control step latency. On LIBERO-Para, it achieves 81.1\% across 4,092 unseen paraphrases and 78.5\% on matched episodes, compared with 36.5\% for SmolVLA LoRA fine-tuned on paraphrases. Without two-step demonstrations, CoBoVLA completes 48.8\% of 160 free-form two-step tasks. Finally, success reaches 91\% when instructions are correctly encoded but falls to 27.2\% otherwise, identifying language understanding as the main source of remaining failures. Together, these results demonstrate that decoupling language understanding and control can improve instruction robustness while making VLA failures easier to diagnose.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/SR-VLA_Stall-Reduced_VLA_Execution_via_Predicted-Input_Lookahead_and_Selective_KV_Cache_Repair|SR-VLA: Stall-Reduced VLA Execution via Predicted-Input Lookahead and Selective KV Cache Repair]]

![[assets/2611.22356_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22356](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22356)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22356
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/SR-VLA_Stall-Reduced_VLA_Execution_via_Predicted-Input_Lookahead_and_Selective_KV_Cache_Repair|SR-VLA: Stall-Reduced VLA Execution via Predicted-Input Lookahead and Selective KV Cache Repair]]
- **作者**: Tao Zhang, Yi Zhong, Jinhao Ruan, Yipeng Gao, Linfeng Zhong, Youming Yang, Yuan Wang
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《SR-VLA: Stall-Reduced VLA Execution via Predicted-Input Lookahead and Selective KV Cache Repair》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, SR-VLA, U-Net。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) policies incur control stalls when inference blocks the next action chunk. Asynchronous early inference can hide latency, but its results may be inconsistent with the arriving actual inputs. Unconditional reuse risks acting on mismatched inputs, whereas full recomputation discards useful precomputation. We propose SR-VLA, combining predicted-input lookahead with selective KV cache repair. During current chunk execution, a lightweight action-conditioned predictor generates next-boundary visual observations and proprioceptive state. These predictions, together with the task instruction, drive the frozen VLA to precompute a speculative KV cache and action chunk. After real inputs are available to VLA, predicted--real differences and task conditions determine whether to accept the action chunk or trigger selective KV repair. On LIBERO, SR-VLA achieves $2.67\times$ and $1.27\times$ query speedups over the respective synchronous $π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA-OFT baselines, while maintaining competitive average task success rates. Under target-motion perturbation, success remains baseline-comparable, with $1.52\times$--$1.93\times$ query speedup.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Beyond_Episodic_Recall_Experience-to-Knowledge_Evolution_for_Lifelong_Embodied_Navigation|Beyond Episodic Recall: Experience-to-Knowledge Evolution for Lifelong Embodied Navigation]]

![[assets/2611.22321_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22321](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22321)

- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Beyond_Episodic_Recall_Experience-to-Knowledge_Evolution_for_Lifelong_Embodied_Navigation|Beyond Episodic Recall: Experience-to-Knowledge Evolution for Lifelong Embodied Navigation]]

- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Beyond Episodic Recall: Experience-to-Knowledge Evolution for Lifelong Embodied Navigation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：GOAT-Bench, LMEE-Bench, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Lifelong embodied navigation requires agents to accumulate experience through continuous interaction and reuse it for subsequent goal searches. Existing memory-enhanced methods typically retain historical observations or construct structured scene representations. However, storing observations or high-dimensional semantic embeddings incurs growing storage costs. Meanwhile, structured representations support retrieval and semantic reasoning but do not explicitly evolve accumulated experience into environment-specific knowledge. To address these limitations, we propose Experience-Evolving Memory (EvoNav), a novel two-timescale memory framework. It organizes semantic observations, spatial relations, and task-feedback cues into sparse experience atoms. During online operation, experience atoms continually consolidate repeated observations and form dynamic links based on transitions. At the slower timescale, EvoNav distills accumulated category co-occurrences and feedback signals into environment-specific structural knowledge without relying on predefined object--room relations. For each new goal, EvoNav combines direct goal evidence from experience atoms with learned structural knowledge to guide goal-directed search on the current traversability map. Experiments on single-goal, sequential multi-floor, and multimodal navigation demonstrate effective experience reuse and improved path efficiency with compact persistent memory, together with successful transfer to a real robot.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Heave-Ho: Decentralized Long-Horizon Multi-Humanoid Collaboration》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HH-Bench, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Collaboration promises to extend humanoid capabilities beyond what individual robots can achieve. However, realizing this potential requires coordinated whole-body interactions, coherent long-horizon planning, and precise synchronization across robots. We introduce Heave-Ho, a decentralized framework for long-horizon collaborative multi-humanoid loco-manipulation. At the low level, we develop a multi-agent post-training framework that adapts a humanoid foundation tracking model to physically coupled interactions between humanoids, enabling coordinated decentralized control from local observations alone. At the high level, we train a vision-language planner and design a speech-based leader-follower coordination mechanism to synchronize decentralized execution without network communication. Trained with two identical humanoids, our system scales to teams of up to ten robots and generalizes to heterogeneous teams without retraining. To benchmark these capabilities, we introduce HH-Bench, comprising 12 tasks involving teams of 2 to 10 humanoids, including heterogeneous teams. Systematic evaluations show that Heave-Ho consistently outperforms baseline methods, while real-world experiments demonstrate robust decentralized collaboration across challenging tasks.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《ALERT: Extending Humanoid Motion Trackers with a General and Lightweight Human Avoidance Pipeline》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Person-MinkUNet, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Whole-body motion tracking has enabled humanoid dancing, athletic motions, and teleoperation, providing a foundation for capability demonstrations and manipulation data collection. However, existing tracking policies lack awareness of nearby humans and proactive avoidance capabilities, while motion substitution and local constraint-based corrections may compromise reference motion integrity and tracking continuity. We propose ALERT, a general lightweight pipeline for extending motion tracking to shared human-robot environments. Through lightweight adaptation, it integrates human perception and proactive avoidance into pretrained tracking policies, balancing avoidance demands with reference motion tracking while preserving existing motion capabilities. Simulation and real-world experiments across tracking backbones demonstrate reliable collision-free execution through localized human avoidance, preserving whole-body coordination and enabling rapid recovery of reference tracking, while retaining the ability of pretrained policies to track unseen motions.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《From Temperature-Limited to Battery-Limited Legged Locomotion》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：结果验证了方案可行性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

On legged robots, sustained locomotion often ends in thermal shutdown rather than battery depletion: the heat of walking concentrates in a few joint motors, which overheat long before the battery is empty. We train thermally aware locomotion policies by reinforcement learning without ever observing temperature, and demonstrate this on a Unitree Go2 quadruped. We first identify a coupled 13-node lumped RC thermal network, fitted on two runs of a single robot and calibrated against the measured electrical power, that predicts all twelve motor temperatures with a mean RMSE below 1 $^\circ$C across different robot instances, gaits, payloads, and speeds. Under a sustained gait, the temperatures the motors will settle at follow directly from the gait-averaged squared torques and joint speeds. Our core contribution is a reward that drives down the hottest predicted asymptotes: thermal time constants exceed the credit-assignment horizon of reinforcement learning by orders of magnitude, but predicted asymptotes respond to torque and joint speed within a single gait cycle, so the policy learns to lower and spread heat with no temperature input. On the robot, the policy turns sustained locomotion from temperature-limited to battery-limited: with a 5 kg payload at 1 m/s, a learned baseline reaches thermal shutdown after 19 minutes (1.1 km), whereas our policy runs the battery down after 174 minutes (10.0 km) with its hottest motor 14 $^\circ$C below the shutdown threshold in a 26 $^\circ$C room; on an unloaded factory circuit with stairs and ramps, the baseline overheats after 25 laps (6.2 km), while our policy completes 47 (11.2 km) on one battery with 22 $^\circ$C of margin.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《CRA: Continuous Risk Assessment for Safety-critical Visual Navigation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；实时应用对效率提出要求；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Traditional safety-critical control methods often rely on predefined constraints that separate states into safe and unsafe regions. While recent studies have extended geometric safety toward semantic safety, real-world safety is rarely purely binary. The risk associated with an object or human can vary continuously with its physical properties, like motion, and interaction context; for example, a person carrying a heavy object or a hot object moving at high speed may require a different safety response from an ordinary static obstacle. In this paper, we propose a vision-based continuous risk assessment framework for safety-critical visual navigation. Our method uses RGB and LiDAR observations to estimate four risk factors (kinetic, potential, contact-severity, and thermal) and combines them into a continuous risk score for each assessed object or person. These estimates are integrated into a Poisson safety function with a Laplace guidance field as a safety filter, enabling online monitoring and intervention in open-world robot navigation. We demonstrate the proposed method in a simulated restaurant scene.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《ALOHA Lightning: Learning Fast and Precise Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Learning from human demonstrations has enabled robots to acquire a wide range of manipulation skills, but learned policies typically execute far slower than ordinary humans. This speed gap is mainly due to lack of an interface for collecting demonstration data at high speed, and the difficulty in training policies that can robustly execute high-speed motions. In this paper, we present ALOHA Lightning (Flash ALOHA), a system for learning fast and precise robotic manipulation. Our system uses kinesthetic teaching to intuitively collect near-human-speed demonstrations on a backdrivable bimanual platform, yielding natural and fast trajectories. We also present a learning pipeline that enables smooth high-speed execution through test-time action smoothing and aligns the visual data distribution between data collection and deployment with masking. Given 50 demonstrations for each task, ALOHA Lightning autonomously completes bimanual tasks such as ball tossing and catching, folding shorts, battery insertion, and bussing tables for over 80% success rates while close to human speed. Project website: https://flash-aloha.github.io/

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《From Human Videos to Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation Data: Confidence-Guided RL Refinement of Video Priors》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：X-Sim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Collecting demonstrations for bimanual dexterous manipulation is costly, motivating the use of human videos as a source of robot training data. However, uncertainty in video-derived motion estimates and differences between human and robot embodiments make direct motion transfer challenging. In this work, we introduce PriDex, a framework that transforms human-video Priors into executable robot Dexterous manipulation demonstrations through reinforcement learning in simulation. PriDex preserves task-relevant interaction structure while allowing embodiment-specific behavior to be regenerated for the robot. Human-object interactions provide affordance cues for selecting task-relevant, robot-specific grasps from geometry-based candidates. For motion refinement, object trajectories provide a task-level reference, while relative bimanual wrist trajectories offer complementary guidance for coordinated motion. Since object-motion estimates can become unreliable under occlusion or visual ambiguity, PriDex uses object-motion confidence to adaptively balance these two priors, emphasizing object-level guidance when confidence is high and increasing the contribution of wrist-level guidance as confidence decreases. Reinforcement learning refines these video-derived priors under the robot's kinematic and physical constraints, and the resulting policies are rolled out to generate robot demonstrations for training a vision-based diffusion policy. We evaluate PriDex on four bimanual dexterous manipulation tasks, including pouring, sweeping into a dustpan, bottle-cap unscrewing, and plate cleaning, assessing both simulation-based robot data generation and downstream visuomotor policy learning.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can recognize graph features yet award credit without sufficient graphical support. This exploratory study examines selected grading outputs for synthetic responses to four kinematics exercises, generated through visual distortion and progressive transformation toward reference graphs. Different model and prompt configurations were used. Some judgments correctly distinguished instantaneous zero velocity from rest over a finite interval and recognized positive curvature. Yet a severely distorted velocity--time graph received full credit despite disconnected and branching traces and an unclear zero-velocity reference. Other submissions graded under the same configuration lost substantial credit. Numerical cutoffs intended to distinguish meaningful features from drawing irregularities were supplied without a coordinate convention for unnumbered graphs, leaving the basis of exact partial-credit awards unresolved. Stress testing can expose unsupported awards before classroom use. For the configurations examined, we do not recommend accepting scores as final grades without instructor review, including review of full-credit responses.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/LMLineage_Content_Lineage_and_Context_Governance_for_LLM_Agents|LMLineage: Content Lineage and Context Governance for LLM Agents]]

![[assets/2611.22511_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22511](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22511)

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL

#### 研究背景与动机

《LMLineage: Content Lineage and Context Governance for LLM Agents》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Interpretability for AI agents should not be confined to understanding the model's internal weights. It must also cover the agent's reasoning and decision-making process over multi-source context. We present LMLineage, an out-of-band system that attributes every output sentence of an agent session to its supporting input spans with calibrated confidences, without requiring white-box access to the model or any additional inference passes. On top of the lineage metadata, we build concept-drift micro compaction: instead of waiting for the context-length limit and summarizing everything, the system detects when previously used input documents have stopped being referenced ("drifted") and surgically compacts only those, preserving user instructions verbatim. We evaluate on a multi-tier corpus: 71 gold annotated sentences from 10 Chinese web-QA cases, plus 90 turns of independently collected multi-turn sessions across three generator models (gpt-4o-mini, qwen3.8-27b, deepseek-v4-flash). Results show that (i) window-level embedding similarity is far better aligned with LLM paraphrasing than whole document or lexical matching; (ii) the greedy-window fast path is both faster and more accurate on average than exhaustive line-matching (a multiple-comparisons bias in the latter inflates web recall at the cost of overall accuracy); (iii) per-turn attribution completes in 1s on a single GPU, remaining stable as context accumulates to 40 turns; and (iv) concept-drift detection is conservative (0% false compaction) with recall bounded by attribution accuracy. We further describe the concept-drift micro compaction design and an interactive incident-tracing/memory-cleaning case study. A companion patent application covers the lineage construction method.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/LMMR_Learned_Multigranular_Memory_Retrieval_for_Small_Language_Model_Agents|LMMR: Learned Multigranular Memory Retrieval for Small Language Model Agents]]

![[assets/2611.22425_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22425](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22425)

- **相关领域**: 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《LMMR: Learned Multigranular Memory Retrieval for Small Language Model Agents》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computation and Language 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HotpotQA, LongBench, LongMemEval, MemoryAgentBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Small language model agents have limited space to read the memory needed for multi-hop questions. Relevant facts are often scattered across records whose surrounding text consumes much of that space. We propose LMMR, a learned multigranular memory retrieval method that selects both the next source and how much of it to read. LMMR links paragraphs and sentence fragments to their original sources and pairs views that supply the same missing support during training. Relevant distractors and repeated information provide contrasting examples. At inference, the reranker scores what each candidate adds to an anchor passage, and a selector chooses the evidence within a token budget. On 22,398 official validation questions from HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, and MuSiQue, a frozen 2B answer model reaches 61.47, 43.26, and 30.58 F1 under a 400-token budget. The gains over query expansion are 3.06, 2.14, and 4.55 points, while memory input decreases by 23.1-26.9%. LMMR also uses 23.5-27.7% fewer input tokens than a coarse retriever trained on the same questions. Against that retriever followed by LLMLingua-2 at comparable input lengths, F1 gains reach 3.99 points and are significant on HotpotQA and 2WikiMultihopQA after Holm correction. All eight small answer models evaluated on HotpotQA improve over query expansion and MMR in both F1 and exact match.

</details>

- **arXiv**: [2611.22342](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22342)

- **作者**: Dongseok Heo, Junghwan Kim, Bongwon Suh
- **cs 子类**: cs.CL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Bad Apples in the Kitchen: How LLM Teams Detect, Correct, and Calibrate Against Their Misaligned Members》归入 大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computation and Language 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

What protects an LLM team when one teammate quietly goes wrong? Most multi-agent LLM work assumes cooperative partners and inspects chat between turns before acting. Real-time embodied cooperation does not allow this pause: chat runs alongside action, so a teammate whose words stay cooperative while behavior diverges can cause damage faster than the team can name what is happening. We study this case by injecting three theory-grounded misaligned agents into a custom four-player Overcooked environment, each stressing a different joint-action layer (speech-action mismatch, role invasion, and aggregate-progress disruption), and decompose robustness into three sequential gates of detection, correction, and calibration through a 3x4x2 design (n=720 episodes). Nearly all trolls are eventually named, but recovery follows only when attribution and situational diagnosis appear in the same utterance; direct source-attribution prompting improves the hardest case only by degrading the troll-free baseline by 37.5%. Robustness, then, depends less on detection alone than on whether team chat combines attribution with diagnosis in a single utterance, suggesting that sensitivity to misalignment should be targeted rather than uniformly raised.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Multi-Agent Memory Inconsistency: Where Factual Conflicts Disappear in LLM Agents》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HiddenBench, HotpotQA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multi-agent delegation lets LLM systems distribute evidence collection across workers with separate contexts. This creates a consistency challenge: facts retrieved by workers may be missing from the evidence available to the lead for a joint conclusion. We introduce a benchmark and evidence-flow profiler for studying multi-agent memory inconsistency through controlled factual conflicts in Harvey LAB document-review tasks. Pooled across three models with comparable generation spending, requiring delegation increases retrieval of both conflicting claims from 50.2% to 68.7% relative to single-agent execution, yet conflict detection falls from 16.2% to 13.2%. Within this setting, workers report both claims for only 35.2% of retrieved pairs, and pooled matched comparisons show higher detection than single-agent execution when one worker reads both claims but lower detection when the claims are split across workers. Worker-to-lead handoffs are a central target for improving conflict detection in multi-agent LLMs.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/GapLeak_When_Privacy-Preserving_LLM-based_Web_Agents_Become_a_Privacy_Risk|GapLeak: When Privacy-Preserving LLM-based Web Agents Become a Privacy Risk]]

![[assets/2611.22223_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22223](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22223)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22223
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/GapLeak_When_Privacy-Preserving_LLM-based_Web_Agents_Become_a_Privacy_Risk|GapLeak: When Privacy-Preserving LLM-based Web Agents Become a Privacy Risk]]
- **作者**: Chaklam Cheong, Tiancong Cao, Qi Li, Zelin Zhang, Jianbing Ni, Rongxing Lu, Mohammad Zulkernine
- **cs 子类**: cs.CR
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.1（加权：大模型 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, Security

#### 研究背景与动机

《GapLeak: When Privacy-Preserving LLM-based Web Agents Become a Privacy Risk》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Cryptography and Security 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large Language Model (LLM)-based web agents rely on data minimization and least privilege to protect user privacy. We show that these principles are necessary but insufficient: they constrain authorized sources, but not higher-sensitivity sources that remain reachable. A website can request a field unavailable from authorized sources, creating an information gap. To complete the task, a goal-driven agent may escalate to reachable but unauthorized sources and disclose accumulated sensitive information. We formalize this vulnerability as GapLeak, where an adversary exploits a legitimate web form without prompt injection or other malicious instructions. We evaluate GapLeak on three web-agent frameworks and six LLMs from Anthropic, Google, and OpenAI across recruitment, e-commerce, travel, and healthcare. GapLeak induces an average unauthorized disclosure of approximately 37%. The information gap is the primary driver: eliminating it reduces disclosure by more than 50%. Existing instruction-level defenses based on least privilege partially mitigate the vulnerability but often reduce task success. We propose submission-time verification, which filters unauthorized content before form submission, eliminating over 96% of unauthorized disclosures while preserving task success.

</details>

![[assets/2611.22177_figure.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《The Only Edge Was Memorized in Training: LLM Trading Agents Around Earnings and Bad News》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language model (LLM) trading agents are tested on news, but news reaches stock prices within minutes, and a benchmark that replays headlines at their publication time finds no alpha in trading on them. Money can then remain in two places, before news whose date is known, such as earnings announcements, and after bad news, if the market's fall went too far. However, studies of LLMs and earnings read documents published with or after the announcement, and studies of reversal sort monthly portfolios, so neither tells whether an agent can trade these moments after costs. In this paper, we test LLM agents at both moments on the same look-ahead-free benchmark, before 598 earnings announcements and after 409 bad-news drops of large US stocks. Every input is cut at the decision close, staged agents are compared with single calls, simple rules and a logistic regression, and every decision is made with and without the stock's name. Across five models from four vendors, no agent beat the simple bets. Before earnings, the agents mostly bought and earned what buying before every announcement earned, and after bad news, a Gemini-based agent called almost every fall an overreaction while no agent picked better dips than buying them all. The only gains came from what the models had memorized in training. Given only the ticker and the dates, a GPT-based model called the direction of 59.2% of the announcements before its training cutoff and 53.3% after it. Agent builders should therefore compare an event agent with the simple bet and test it blind after the training cutoff.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Safety cases are widely used to demonstrate that mission-critical systems, such as autonomous vehicles and unmanned aerial systems, can operate safely after deployment. However, manually constructing safety cases is complex, time-consuming, and labor-intensive, which calls for the proposal of automated approaches to support their creation. Inspired by the legal nature of safety case construction, we formalize the safety case creation problem through a courtroom analogy, where stakeholders collaboratively propose, examine, criticize, review, improve and validate safety claims and their supporting evidence. In this analogy, the safety analyst acts as a prosecutor by identifying hazards and safety concerns, while the assurance case developer serves as a defense attorney by constructing and substantiating safety arguments. An orchestrator acts as a judge by coordinating stakeholder interactions, and a review board serves as a jury by assessing argument completeness and persuasiveness. Based on this formalization, we propose eTrustWare, a Large Language Model (LLM)-based multi-agent framework for automated safety case generation and review. Our experiments on several safety cases of ML-enabled autonomous driving systems demonstrate the feasibility of coordinated LLM-based agents for generating safety cases.

</details>

《Does Your Agent Need to Know Its Budget? A Placebo-Controlled Experiment on LLM Coding Agents》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Software Engineering 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Organizations deploying autonomous coding agents impose token budgets, yet the causal effects of a budget, and of telling the agent about it, are unmeasured. We conduct a placebo-controlled factorial experiment under a frozen, timestamped protocol, crossing budget enforcement with budget disclosure on two executable program-repair benchmarks (5,835 runs, two open models, three cap levels). Enforcing a calibrated cap reduced task resolution by 20.0 percentage points while reducing tokens per resolved task roughly twelvefold. Disclosing the enforced budget never helped: the placebo-controlled risk difference was -2.8 pp (95% CI [-6.2, +0.7]) at the medium cap and -4.2 pp ([-8.1, -0.2], nominally significant, unadjusted) at the tight cap, with every prompt wording and per-turn feedback variant far below the 10-point effect size of interest. Observed run-to-run outcome variance was roughly 1.5x the uncapped level under caps, though part of this reflects resolution rates moving nearer one half. These results make budget governance for coding agents an enforcement-design problem: no communication variant produced an effect beyond that of an unrelated sentence, which itself raised resolution by 7.2 pp (95% CI [+4.0, +10.5]) over silent enforcement, a sentence effect that only a placebo control removes.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《LLM Trading Agents Earn Beta, Not Alpha: A Look-Ahead-Free Benchmark》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：InvestorBench, LiveTradeBench, StockBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language model (LLM) trading agents now trade stocks for their users, and benchmarks rank models by their agents' returns. Yet a single run's return says little about skill. In our study, two runs of the same model that differed only in the random seed ended 5.2 points apart on average, so telling skill from luck takes many backtests across markets, models and seeds. However, live arenas trade each day only once, and backtests either use the few months after a model's training cutoff or run on years the model may remember, often with news joined to prices by calendar date. In this paper, we present rta-bench-1, a backtest over three US markets (a rally, a bear market and a crash). It shows each headline only after its publication minute, plays each period blind and named, and checks every run for look-ahead. It also splits each return into factor exposure and alpha and scores every decision against random choices of the same day and sector. In 91 runs of six models from four vendors, no alpha was significant, market and style exposure accounted for a median 78% of the return, and the decisions did no better than random stocks of the same sector. The agents bought the stocks with the most positive news, yet in ablations they earned as much without their headlines. Still, the agents carried out rules reliably, since a single cash rule in the prompt cut a GPT-based agent's share in stocks by 30.3 points in all six pairs. Builders should therefore supply the edge themselves and report exposures and alpha, and benchmarks should replay news by publication time.

</details>

《Learning to Undo: Transfer Reinforcement Learning under State Space Transformations》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Transfer learning in reinforcement learning (RL) has shown strong empirical success. In this work, we take a more principled perspective by studying when and how transferring knowledge between MDPs (a source and a target) can be provably beneficial. Specifically, we consider the case where there exists an undo map such that applying this map to the target's state space recovers the source exactly. We propose an algorithm that learns this map by matching state feature statistics gathered from both MDPs, and then uses it to transfer the source policy to the target. We theoretically justify the algorithm by analyzing the setting when the undo map is linear and the source is linearly-$Q^\star$ realizable, where our approach has strictly better sample complexity than tabula rasa RL in the target MDP. Empirically, we demonstrate that these benefits extend beyond this regime: on challenging continuous control tasks and Atari games, our method achieves significantly better sample efficiency. Overall, our results highlight how shared structure between tasks can be leveraged for efficient transfer of policies across environments.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Deep Reinforcement Learning for Heat Pump and Underfloor Heating Control: An End-to-End Case Study》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Efficient control of underfloor heating (UFH) systems coupled with heat pumps (HPs) is challenging due to the high thermal inertia of buildings and the slow dynamic response of UFH systems. Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers a data-driven control approach capable of simultaneously optimizing energy consumption and thermal comfort. However, the application of DRL-based controllers in occupied buildings under real operating conditions remains limited. This work presents and end-to-end case study (development, evaluation, and real-world deployment) of a DRL-based controller for an integrated UFH-HP system. First, a simulation-based pilot study was conducted to assess different DRL algorithms, control strategies, and configurations. Subsequently, a digital twin of a real residential building was developed and used to train DRL agents under simulated operating conditions. The best-performing agent was then deployed in the physical building without additional online fine-tuning. The results show that the controller successfully maintained indoor temperatures within the prescribed comfort limits while dynamically adjusting the HP supply temperature and UFH valve positions. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of deploying DRL controllers in occupied buildings and highlight their robustness and the practical challenges associated with transferring control policies from digital environments to real heating systems.

</details>
