#### 研究背景与动机

《When Anchors Shift the Target: Advantage-Gated Adaptation for Diffusion Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Forward-process reinforcement learning adapts diffusion and flow-matching models by re-noising rewarded generations into regression targets. Because negative advantages induce concave updates with unbounded repulsive gradients, existing methods stabilize training using cached predictions from a moving policy copy, yet these anchors also alter what positive feedback teaches the model to fit. We formalize this conflict as anchor coupling: after separating frozen-reference regularizers, affine moving-anchor rules cannot eliminate negative curvature while preserving every positive regression target. We propose Advantage-Gated Adaptation (AGA) to decouple these roles. Positive samples retain standard target fitting, whose self-limiting update vanishes at the observed target independently of the moving anchor. Negative samples instead use the first-order tangent of the concave objective at the moving-policy prediction, preserving the local repulsion direction with a prediction-independent gradient. Within the quadratic-anchor family, these requirements uniquely determine the parameter-free gate $ω(a)=\max(-a,0)$. Because the moving copy provides local linearization rather than retention, AGA assigns long-horizon behavior preservation to a frozen pretrained reference regularized at its operating classifier-free guidance scale, interpreted through an implicit-reward lens. Across compositional generation, text rendering, and preference alignment, our experiments show that displacing positive targets degrades visual quality and heightens sensitivity to policy-copy schedules, whereas AGA consistently improves the reward--quality trade-off.

</details>

- **作者**: Jiaqi Wu, Yongqi Dong, Yijie Zhong, Maple Shao, Yide Tao, Linsen Cai, Weize Cai, Hai L. Vu, Alvaro Garcia Hernandez

#### 研究背景与动机

《Residual-Guided Lightweight Reinforcement Learning for Risk-Aware Trajectory Selection with a Frozen World Model》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ResWorld, RiskWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

In automated driving, world models provide future scene predictions for evaluating candidate trajectories, but fixed selection rules may not capture scene-dependent trade-offs. We propose a residual-guided reinforcement learning (RL) framework for lightweight trajectory selection with a frozen world model and a fixed candidate bank. The framework uses proximal policy optimization (PPO) to fine-tune a joint policy over candidate selection and bounded trajectory residuals. A context-conditioned ranking network learns candidate preferences from feedback obtained by evaluating local perturbations of the selected trajectories. At inference, the selector combines learned preference and predicted risk ranks using fixed weights and returns the selected candidate trajectory without modification. Fine-tuning updates 16,106 parameters across the actor and critic, while the deployed selector retains only the 11,725-parameter ranking network, with the residual branch and critic used exclusively during training. In open-loop evaluation on the nuScenes validation set, the method reduces average Euclidean trajectory error (L2) by 3.99% relative to the RiskWorld model baseline without RL-based selection. Under matched budgets for feedback accesses and actor updates, residual exploration reduces L2 by 0.83% relative to the categorical PPO variant trained without residuals. The method also maintains lower L2 than RiskWorld when risk features are removed or shuffled.

</details>

- **作者**: Tung Dang, The Hung Phung, Son Lam Nguyen, Tu Nguyen
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习

#### 研究背景与动机

《Action-outcome coupling: a pre-investment feasibility audit for offline reinforcement learning in supply chain operations》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Machine Learning 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Prescriptive analytics initiatives in logistics, such as offline reinforcement learning and next-best-action systems, are often funded before anyone verifies that the available historical logs can support an improved decision policy. We formalise this pre-investment feasibility question via action-outcome coupling: a total-effect variance index measuring how much expected outcomes change with the logged action. Coupling provides an algorithm-independent upper bound on any policy's achievable value, expressed in monetary units per decision for both single-step and discounted sequential objectives. We also analyse the asymptotic false-licensing risk of an invalid workflow: fitting a value model and evaluating the model's greedy policy on the same data. The false-licence rate is driven mainly by the number of state cells over which selection is performed, rising from the nominal test level with one cell to near certainty with a few dozen, while overlap has a smaller effect. We then propose an audit that assigns logs to four tiers of decision support, separately for fixed and contextual policies, and returns an explicit stopping rationale. On a randomised e-commerce log, the audit endorses a fixed recommendation policy, certifying gains equal to 30 per cent of the theoretical ceiling; the remaining gap reflects bound looseness under an 80-action design. Two public logistics logs fail at different checkpoints: one because the target is an accounting identity of the action, and another because, with 27 clusters, estimated coupling stays below the design's detectability threshold even though a conservative offline learner certifies an improvement in 29 of 30 seeds.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Robust Actor-Critic Learning under Transport and Divergence Uncertainty》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Learning reliable control policies requires accounting for uncertainty in transition dynamics and errors introduced by function estimation and action generation. We develop an actor--critic method for discounted robust Markov decision processes with continuous states and actions and an unknown nominal transition law. We consider transition uncertainty sets defined by Wasserstein distance, total variation, Kullback--Leibler divergence, or Pearson chi-square divergence. A conditional diffusion model is fitted once to offline transitions. At each subsequent iteration, we estimate the robust action-value function from current-policy observations using a shared-target minimax criterion, then update a generative actor through exponentially weighted score matching. Regularization preserves exploration and provides the density regularity needed for conditional distribution estimation. Under coverage and regularity conditions, we establish finite-sample bounds for critic estimation, the learned conditional action law, and the robust return of the implemented policy. The bounds distinguish critic estimation error, uncertainty in the nominal transition model, and policy regularization, and characterize how the four robustness radii affect the resulting guarantees.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Eyes in the Update? Learning Where Rewards Land in Multimodal Reasoning》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Gaze-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multimodal post-training has moved from broad instruction tuning toward reinforcement learning for image and video reasoning, where outcome rewards can improve final-answer accuracy. Yet these rewards are assigned to complete generated trajectories, while visual evidence is used only at sparse and uneven positions inside the response. Recent work has begun to make multimodal RL more token-sensitive through grounding signals or external token-level weighting. We ask a more specific question: can the allocation of the same trajectory-level reward be inferred from the model's own multimodal computation, rather than from an auxiliary annotator, perception module, or process label? We propose Gaze-RL, a representation-driven policy optimization method that redistributes completion-level advantage through a latent reward landing field. The field is computed from internal cross-modal geometry and local predictive uncertainty, assigning larger update weight to positions that are both visually anchored and decision-bearing while remaining mean-one, so the total credit of each rollout is preserved and only its positionwise allocation changes. Gaze-RL requires no process supervision, auxiliary perception module, or architectural modification, and improves both Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3.5 across a broad range of image and video benchmarks.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《More Than a Score: Reinforcement Learning with Verbalized Feedback for Generative Recommendation》归入 强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Information Retrieval 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for generative recommendation, where recommended items are generated directly from users' interaction histories. To further improve recommendation quality, reinforcement learning (RL) has been applied to align these models with recommendation objectives. However, existing RL methods rely on scalar rewards to implicitly guide exploration. These rewards do not specify which aspects of a candidate conflict with the user's preferences or how the next candidate should differ. To address this gap, we propose VerRec, a verbalized feedback-guided RL framework for generative recommendation. Specifically, a user simulator generates personalized verbalized feedback based on the user's historical preferences and the candidate item profile. The policy uses this feedback to produce refined recommendations. Original and refined candidates form an expanded group and are jointly optimized with group relative policy optimization. The user simulator is only required during training and is removed at inference time. Experiments on three Amazon review datasets with two generative recommendation backbones demonstrate that VerRec improves recommendation accuracy and ranking quality. Further analyses show that verbalized guidance encourages exploration of more diverse candidates and helps the policy discover more rewarding recommendations during training.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《EviDARL: Evidence-Centric Dual-Agent Reasoning via Alternating Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Table Question Answering》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EviDARL, MMQA, MultiTabQA, TQA-Bench, TQABench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Despite advances in LLM-based table reasoning, most studies focus on single-table settings. Multi-table QA requires multi-hop reasoning over complex table relationships and evidence dispersed across long contexts, often without verifiable intermediate traces. To address these challenges, we introduce EviDA, an evidence-centric dual-agent framework comprising an Evidence Construction Agent and a Reasoning and Answering Agent. The Evidence Construction Agent generates and executes table-manipulation programs over complete tables to produce question-specific evidence and verifiable intermediate traces, simplifying subsequent reasoning. The Reasoning and Answering Agent then performs multi-hop reasoning over this evidence to derive final answers. We further introduce EviDARL, which combines agent-specific fine-grained rewards with parameter-efficient alternating reinforcement learning to improve collaboration between the agents. By optimizing each agent in turn while holding the other fixed, EviDARL enables bidirectional adaptation between evidence construction and answer reasoning. Experiments on MMQA and TQA-Bench demonstrate that EviDARL substantially outperforms competitive open-model and multi-table reasoning baselines under constrained model and training budgets.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Discovery of new antiviral agents through artificial intelligence: in vitro and in vivo results》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Machine Learning 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

In this research, we employed a deep reinforcement learning (RL)-based molecule design platform to generate a diverse set of compounds targeting the neuraminidase (NA) of influenza A and B viruses. A total of 60291 compounds were generated, of which 86.5% displayed superior physicochemical properties compared to oseltamivir. After narrowing down the selection through computational filters, nine compounds with non-sialic acid-like structures were selected for in vitro experiments. We identified two compounds, DS-22-inf-009 and DS-22-inf-021 that effectively inhibited the NAs of both influenza A and B viruses (IAV and IBV), including H275Y mutant strains at low micromolar concentrations. Molecular dynamics simulations revealed a similar pattern of interaction with amino acid residues as oseltamivir. In cell-based assays, DS-22-inf-009 and DS-22-inf-021 inhibited IAV and IBV in a dose-dependent manner with EC50 values ranging from 0.29 uM to 2.31 uM. Furthermore, animal experiments showed that both DS-22-inf-009 and DS-22-inf-021 exerted antiviral activity in mice, conferring 65% and 85% protection from IAV (H1N1 pdm09), and 65% and 100% protection from IBV (Yamagata lineage), respectively. Thus, these findings demonstrate the potential of RL to generate compounds with promising antiviral properties.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Model-based offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) enables sample-efficient adaptation by leveraging offline pre-training for online fine-tuning. However, the distribution shifts between offline and online stages often hinder fine-tuning performance. Many existing methods encode the optimism-pessimism trade-off through a single-objective formulation. Selecting its trade-off coefficient through online fine-tuning can require costly bi-level optimization. We identify the balance established during offline training as a key challenge: in our motivating experiments, optimistic policies adapt better to novel tasks, whereas pessimistic policies perform better on the same online tasks. To address this challenge, we propose a bi-objective formulation that captures this trade-off, yielding a Pareto policy pool during offline training. This policy pool enables adaptive selection during online fine-tuning across diverse online tasks. To generate the pool, we introduce Multi-Objective Soft Actor-critIC (MOSAIC), which solves bi-objective problems and constructs diverse Pareto policies. After offline training, a contextual bandit algorithm hierarchically selects the most suitable policy for fine-tuning at each online interaction step. Empirically, our pipeline, Hierarchical Pareto Policy Pool (HiP3), achieves the strongest aggregate performance across both same and novel online tasks and maintains its advantage under stronger distribution shifts. Comprehensive ablations further clarify the roles of the policy pool, MOSAIC-based pool construction, and the online selection mechanism.

</details>

---

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/When_does_deep_reinforcement_learning_pay_off_in_HVAC_supervisory_control_Four_small_Modelica_case_studies_trained_directly_on_FMUs|When does deep reinforcement learning pay off in HVAC supervisory control? Four small Modelica case studies trained directly on FMUs]]

![[assets/2611.22144_figure.png|800]]

Four small Modelica case studies trained directly on FMUs]]
- **作者**: Bingnan Zhao, Harshala Gammulle, Tharindu Fernando
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG

- **关联关键词**: Agent, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《When does deep reinforcement learning pay off in HVAC supervisory control? Four small Modelica case studies trained directly on FMUs》归入 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：BOPTEST-Gym, DRL, ModelicaGym。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) is often proposed for the supervisory control of heating, ventilation and air-conditioning (HVAC) systems. The reported gains are, however, difficult to interpret, because the baselines are often weak and the attainable optimum is seldom known. This paper examines four small, reproducible case studies based on an office building in Brisbane, Australia: pre-cooling under a time-of-use tariff; chiller staging with condenser water reset; chilled water supply temperature reset with a wet cooling coil; and chilled water storage under time-of-use prices and a demand charge. The plants were modelled with the Modelica Buildings library, and in three of the cases a soft actor-critic agent was trained directly on the exported functional mock-up unit (FMU). Each agent was compared with tuned rule-based controllers and, where practicable, with a near-optimal reference from hourly exhaustive search or dynamic programming with perfect foresight. In the two cases without storage the agent reached the near-optimal reference, but the margin above a well-chosen rule was small: 1.3% for condenser water reset and 0.18% for chilled water reset. In the latter case two common reset rules consumed more energy than a fixed set point. With a chilled water storage tank the margin was considerably larger. The agent outperformed both rules (-7.8%, compared with -3.9% and -6.3%) and adopted midday charging during the solar off-peak period, yet it obtained only about 45% of the saving achieved by the perfect-foresight schedule and did little to limit the demand peak. In the pre-cooling case a hand-tuned rule remained superior (-12.0% compared with -5.6%). These results suggest that studies of DRL for HVAC control should state the margin between the best rule and a near-optimal reference. Some practical difficulties encountered in training on co-simulation FMUs are also reported.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Addressing Exogenous Variability in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MARL, Meta-RL, PEARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) often fails under exogenous variability such as shifts in opponents or environment regimes that cannot be controlled by the team but alter the transition dynamics. We formalize this challenge as Exogenous Dec-POMDP (ED-POMDP), in which an episode-level exogenous regime, selected independently of the team, indexes the dynamics, and the global state is partitioned into components that are directly responsive to the current team action and components that are not directly action-responsive at the current step. This formulation exposes mediated influence: while team actions do not directly update the latter at the current step, they can shape their later evolution through induced changes in the former. Based on this, we propose LEICA, a CTDE-compatible algorithm that learns history-conditioned context representations for both components and shapes policy updates using agent-wise, influence-weighted counterfactual intrinsic rewards. Across SMAX benchmarks with opponent strategy shifts, LEICA consistently improves both training performance and generalization to unseen opponents' strategies over existing baselines, supporting the usefulness of influence-weighted shaping under exogenous train-test regime shifts.

</details>

- **作者**: Song Yan, Chenfeng Wang, Wei Zhai, Xingchen Wang, Tao Zhang, Yawen Shao, Shi Pan, Min Li, Zheng-Jun Zha

#### 研究背景与动机

《RiDEx: Reward-Informed Directional Exploration for Diffusion Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ExploreNet, GenEval。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reward-based diffusion post-training updates model parameters from task rewards. While existing approaches improve sampling and policy optimization, they still rely largely on isotropic random perturbations in a high-dimensional latent space for exploration. Such variation often spends the perturbation budget on changes with little influence on the target reward, making exploration inefficient. We study where diffusion models should explore and how to learn from that exploration. We introduce Reward-Informed Directional Exploration (RiDEx), which concentrates local exploration in reward-relevant subspaces of the denoising velocity field. Conditional residuals or differentiable reward gradients propose the subspace, and equal-and-opposite perturbations at the same generation state measure its terminal reward response. The reward difference supplies signed local supervision in the explored coordinates, while all generated images contribute to global DiffusionNFT learning. RiDEx thereby couples exploration-space selection with feedback about the changes it produces. On Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, RiDEx attains the highest peak rewards among the evaluated methods on four alignment and preference tasks. Its gradient-direction instantiation achieves 15x and 29x target-attainment speedups over DiffusionNFT on HPSv2 and PickScore, measured in H200 GPU hours. Controlled experiments confirm that reward-aligned directions elicit stronger responses at equal perturbation magnitude, and that applying their measured credit improves curve-average reward across all four tasks.

</details>

《RLDE: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Interpretable Iterative Document Editing》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Ptr-Net。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Improving the quality, clarity, and flow of long documents remains a challenging problem in NLP, as human editing typically occurs through iterative refinement rather than a single-pass rewrite. Most existing automated systems act as black-box generators or rely on supervised learning, limiting interpretability and failing to capture the sequential nature of revisions. In this paper, we propose RLDE, an automated end-to-end metric-driven RL framework that formulates document revision as a reference-free MDP problem (unsupervised with respect to paired document-revision targets). Working with a limited action space (NOOP, INSERT, DELETE, REPLACE), RLDE leverages Pointer Networks for dynamic span selection at the token level and sends generative actions to the frozen BART-base model to obtain context-aware suggestions. PPO agents learn to optimize a multi-objective reward function based on readability, perplexity, and grammar metrics with the help of a novel SBERT-based Semantic Preservation Penalty constraint to avoid deletion degeneracy. Evaluations on the BEA-2019 dataset showed statistically significant results (p &lt; 0.01) across all metrics, where Flesch Reading Ease increased from 49.8 to 59.4, perplexity decreased from 63.7 to 39.8, while preserving 93% of semantic information. When compared to Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompt-engineered models like GPT-4 and LLaMA-2, RLDE shows high performance at a very low computational inference cost. Moreover, the quality of edits generated by RLDE has been verified via human evaluations, scoring 4.9/5 for meaning preservation and 4.4/5 for edit quality.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《GP--RL--MPC: Uncertainty-Aware Reinforcement Learning-Augmented Model Predictive Control with Gaussian Process Postdecision State-Value Functions》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We propose Gaussian Process Reinforcement Learning-Augmented Model Predictive Control (GP--RL--MPC), which uses a learned critic as the MPC terminal penalty to account for costs beyond the prediction horizon. Gaussian process regression (GPR) enables data-efficient critic learning from limited, noisy rollouts within optimistic approximate policy iteration (OAPI), with soft updates mitigating policy chattering. A tunable risk coefficient weights GP epistemic uncertainty in the terminal cost, regulating sensitivity to uncertain critic predictions. The postdecision state formulation shifts disturbance averaging offline to account for aleatoric uncertainty from the terminal transition onward. Under matching objectives and admissible actions, a one-step horizon recovers greedy value-space RL. After fitting GP hyperparameters by exact marginal likelihood, we construct a sparse deterministic training conditional (DTC) posterior using inducing points selected by pivoted Cholesky (PC), reducing online evaluation costs as the training dataset grows. In a continuous stirred-tank reactor (CSTR) subject to exogenous disturbances, GP--RL--MPC with an appropriately selected risk coefficient and policy screening reduces the sum of squared errors (SSE) by an average of 80.52% relative to an otherwise matched baseline MPC across five random seeds (sample standard deviation: 3.48%).

</details>

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We develop a policy-gradient (PG) reinforcement learning (RL) framework with hard inter-sample control barrier function (CBF)-based safety constraints for nonlinear sampled-data systems. We first design a class of stochastic policies with support truncated to sampling-period-dependent safe control sets, that guarantee safety-by-construction at sampling instants. We then propose a model-free PG algorithm that searches over the class of safe-by-design policies and establish sufficient conditions on sampling-period such that any action sampled from safe control sets remains safe throughout the sampling interval, providing inter-sample guarantees. We further establish finite-time convergence and sample complexity guarantees for the proposed PG algorithm and characterize its global optimality under gradient dominance. Finally, we illustrate inter-sample safety guarantees on a safe quadrotor navigation environment.

</details>

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Birds are modeled as environmentally sensitive agents whose movement reflects real-world air quality conditions, where AQI serves as a measurable world feedback signal representing pollution-driven consequences. Instead of relying on human preference or synthetic rewards, the system uses environmental outcomes as the primary training signal, enabling policy learning grounded in ecological impact. We propose a multi-instance reinforcement learning simulation in which agents navigate a 2D grid representation of India integrated with a precomputed AQI matrix and geographic state boundaries. The framework combines bird call metadata from the Xeno-canto dataset, spatial discretization, and real environmental data to learn adaptive navigation strategies under pollution constraints. To ensure scalability and efficiency, repeated real-time API queries are replaced by a structured AQI mapping that enables fast lookup during training and execution. Multiple baselines, including Monte Carlo, Q-Learning, and Value Iteration, are evaluated for comparison. Deep Q-Network demonstrates stronger stability, adaptability, and cumulative performance across varying environmental conditions, establishing a scalable foundation for world-feedback driven ecological decision modeling.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Adaptive_Memory_for_Online_Reinforcement_Learning_under_Sparse_Model_Changes|Adaptive Memory for Online Reinforcement Learning under Sparse Model Changes]]

![[assets/2611.21879_first_page.png|800]]

- **作者**: Sreejeet Maity, Sukanya Modak, Leonardo F. Toso
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 世界模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Adaptive Memory for Online Reinforcement Learning under Sparse Model Changes》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Meta-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We study the discounted reinforcement learning (RL) problem in a setting where the underlying environment changes between deployments. Each deployment provides $H$ observations from a fixed MDP. The reward function and transition kernel are fixed within each deployment. Across deployments, at most an $ε&gt;0$ fraction of the reward and transition kernel entries may change. In this setting, learning each model to deploy from scratch may discard useful observations from past deployments, whereas pooling all past observations can bias the estimates of entries that have already changed. We then propose an algorithm that for each state and action pair, selects how much history to reuse to estimate the reward and probabilities of the next state. We start with observations from the current deployment and add earlier deployment observations one at a time, the most recent first. After each addition, we check whether a single average reward agrees with the estimates from all histories considered until that point. We apply the same check to the distribution of next states. For each estimate, we leverage the last history that passes these aforementioned checks. The optimal control policy is then computed on top of the fitted MDP. We characterize the average gap between the optimal return and the return of the learner's policies over a finite number of deployments. We demonstrate that when $0&lt; |S|ε\leq 2$, with $|S|$ being the cardinality of the state space, our algorithm needs only the observations from the most recent $O(1/ε)$ deployments, and achieves a long-run average gap of at most $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{ε/H})$.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Making the World React Realistically: A Closed-Loop Interactive World Model for Autonomous Driving》归入 世界模型、强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MARL, U-Net。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models (WMs) generate driving scenarios beyond recorded logs, enabling the training and evaluation of autonomous driving systems in unseen interactions. However, existing WMs struggle to ensure both behavioral realism and visual fidelity, since they overlook the relationship between vehicle interactions and the ego visual observations. We propose a closed-loop interactive WM that couples behavior modeling and visual rendering. First, imitation-learned background-vehicle (BV) policies are fine-tuned with multi-agent reinforcement learning in the closed loop against an end-to-end ego planner that reacts to the rendered images, so that their interaction behaviors match those in real logs. Second, the viewpoints exposed by these interactions guide a per-Gaussian diagnosis of each 3D Gaussian vehicle asset, which attenuates view-dependent colors on under-observed Gaussians and fills never-observed surfaces with generated Gaussians, while preserving the observed appearance. Third, signal lights are rendered according to the driving intentions of the vehicles, by an editor that learns the light states unobserved for a vehicle through counterfactual fine-tuning with a light-state classifier. On long-tail closed-loop scenarios, our method reduces the divergence from real-world interaction behaviors by 85% and improves novel-view vehicle PSNR by 2.0dB over the state-of-the-art WM. The significance of BV light signaling in WMs is shown, as the rendered signal lights conveying BV driving intentions indirectly impact ego driving behaviors in a quarter of the scenarios.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Beyond the Camera: A Tri-Modal Driving World Model for Future RGB, Thermal, and LiDAR Forecasting in Adverse Weather》归入 世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DrivingWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Driving world models forecast what a vehicle's sensors will record next. Most forecast camera video and some add LiDAR, but thermal cameras, which still see in darkness and some adverse weather, are rarely a forecast target, and to our knowledge no world model forecasts all three sensors. We study joint forecasting of RGB, thermal, and LiDAR on real adverse-weather sequences of the DSERT-RoLL dataset. We post-train a pretrained video world model for each sensor and represent LiDAR as a depth video aligned with the RGB camera, with densification and encoding chosen by what the model's video tokenizer can reconstruct. A tri-sensor model sees all three sensors as one video. We score LiDAR in meters against measured returns and compare against persistence baselines passed through the same tokenizer as the model. With this matching, an apparent loss of the thermal model to persistence in fog disappears. On all 58 validation sequences in six weather conditions, post-trained RGB and thermal forecasts beat compression-matched persistence. Joint training lowers the LiDAR error at 2s by 2.1m at a small cost in camera fidelity, and only the tri-sensor model's LiDAR forecasts beat persistence in mean error. Blanking one sensor degrades the forecasts of the others more in snow than in fog.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Plausible Videos Can Lead to Poor Decisions: Diagnosing and CURE-ing World Models for Planning》归入 世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：PhyWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Action-conditioned world models are increasingly used as world models for planning, yet a world model that makes plausible, realistic-looking videos that are consistent with the training data can produce poor decisions. We address two key questions: (1) when can we trust a world model for planning, and (2) what should we do when we cannot? We introduce CURE (Certify, Upweight, Rollout, Explore), a certificate-guided technique that assesses whether a world model can be trusted for planning and determines what to do next: whether to upweight the training data to debias the model; roll out more samples at test time; or explore by collecting new data where support (how well the training data cover the planner's decisions) is missing. We deploy CURE across multiple domains, spanning simulated physics based on PhyWorld, MuJoCo arm control, and Language-Table pushing in simulation and on real videos. The decisions our certificate trusts have lower regret than those it does not (e.g., 0.06 vs. 0.38 on Language-Table sim and 0.07 vs. 0.38 on MuJoCo contact), whereas video perceptual quality does not identify good decisions. By focusing on decision quality rather than perceptual quality, CURE lowers regret over the best pessimistic decision rule by 10-25% across support levels, and over world-action models, search and policy learning by 41-64%, given the same data.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/JointGV_Structure-Aware_Correction_of_Visual_World_Models_with_Parallel_Graph_Dynamics_for_Long-Horizon_Modeling|JointGV: Structure-Aware Correction of Visual World Models with Parallel Graph Dynamics for Long-Horizon Modeling]]

![[assets/2611.22543_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22543](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22543)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22543
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/JointGV_Structure-Aware_Correction_of_Visual_World_Models_with_Parallel_Graph_Dynamics_for_Long-Horizon_Modeling|JointGV: Structure-Aware Correction of Visual World Models with Parallel Graph Dynamics for Long-Horizon Modeling]]
- **作者**: Tian Lyu, Yichen Jiang, Ziyuan Cheng, Yunda Yan, Xiangyu Pan, Junjie Jiang, Jialin Yu, Boyu Ning, Pietro Liò, Fan Mo
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 世界模型
- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 1.8（加权：具身智能 0.3，世界模型 1，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics, WorldModel

#### 研究背景与动机

《JointGV: Structure-Aware Correction of Visual World Models with Parallel Graph Dynamics for Long-Horizon Modeling》归入 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DAFT-RL, GeoWorld, IRASim, PhyDNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Visual world models predict future visual states conditioned on actions, but recursive errors in entity poses and spatial relations limit their long-horizon prediction for robot planning and control. We ask whether a separately predicted relational state can correct this drift without restructuring the visual model. This is challenging because graph nodes and visual states share neither elements nor a feature space. We propose Joint Graph--Visual Dynamics Correction (JointGV), a plug-in based on a cross-granularity dynamics hypothesis: despite their different representations, graph-node and visual-state changes describe the same physical transition. JointGV rolls out a task-conditioned scene graph alongside the visual world model, couples their predicted changes, and combines the resulting graph-motion context with graph-predicted node positions to compute a bounded residual. Graph-learning and correction gradients do not propagate into the original visual dynamics. We attach JointGV to DINO-WM (spatial tokens) and DreamerV3 (a global latent state), evaluating both on DMC Cheetah Run and Walker Walk across three seeds and extending the evaluation to contact-rich manipulation with DMC Finger Turn Hard. Across 32-step rollouts, JointGV improves structural and visual-prediction accuracy, reducing joint-position error by 11--28\%. For spatial tokens, learned graph--visual change coupling gives lower error than broadcasting the same mean graph-motion context to every token, particularly when non-adjacent parts appear close together in the image. On real-robot inverted-pendulum sequences, JointGV reduces DINO-WM's 32-step pole-angle error from $12.56^\circ$ to $6.69^\circ$ and improves DreamerV3 and LeWorldModel as well. In simulated inverted-pendulum control, an actor trained with JointGV-corrected rollouts completes 46 of 50 episodes, compared with none using the original rollouts.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Testing World Models on Actions They Have Never Seen: Held-Out Plans, Forecast Accuracy and Price Rules in Retail》归入 世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A world model is trusted to answer "what if": how its world responds to an action it has never seen. We give a test of that trust for any action-conditioned world model: actions held out of training by design and scored against a known answer; whether forecast accuracy predicts accuracy on those actions; and the same structure given to simpler models. We run it in retail, where the action is a price and promotion plan, across 2,853 fitted models. First, forecast accuracy is a fair guide between kinds of model (rank correlation with error on new plans 0.32 to 0.66) and a poor guide between versions of one world model: where merchants plan into high-demand weeks the link reverses, down to -0.69. Second, training the world model to learn the effect of actions (JEPA pretraining, a latent consistency check, adaptation at the plan date) leaves its error on new plans unchanged. Third, in generated worlds the recorded regular price and three price rules improve it. Given the same rules, a log-linear model is best when its curve can match the truth; in a world whose curve lies outside the rules, the world model with the rules is ahead of the log-linear model with the rules in 36 of 40 worlds, and does as well without its recurrent cell. On real data (Breakfast at the Frat, The Complete Journey and M5) the rules lower the world model's error on pure price cuts deeper than any it saw (0.63 to 0.56 on the Frat; 0.45 to 0.44, within chance, on The Complete Journey), but not on lasting price moves. Fourth, a small share of random test plans helps every model a little. In a generated dosing world, a stress test in a second domain, the first finding repeats, and the built-up drug level lowers the world model's error (2.86 to 1.91) though it still predicts only 0.12 to 0.32 of the true gain. The test carries to any domain where a world model is asked about actions outside its history.

</details>

- **作者**: Raluca Stevenson, David Bignell, Jennifer C. White, Tabish Rashid, Emanuele Aiello, Mariya Hendriksen, Junha Hyung, Sadegh Aliakbarian, Abdelhak Lemkhenter, Marko Tot, Katja Hofmann, Sarah Parisot

#### 研究背景与动机

《Towards Data-Efficient, Real-Time World Models》归入 世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MineWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

State-of-the-art world models have been used to produce sequences of gameplay that accord with provided user-input actions. However, high quality, consistent gameplay generation often comes at the cost of inference speed, making real-time interactive play and ideation challenging. The substantial quantities of data used to train state-of-the-art models have also made custom training of these models appear impractical, limiting many of their potential uses in creative fields. In this work we demonstrate a major step towards efficient world models: achieving both fast high-quality inference, and improved data efficiency. This is done by replacing the next-token prediction paradigm with a two-stage discrete diffusion-based approach, introducing a lightweight refinement transformer which carries out iterative masked predictions. Building on an autoregressive world model capable of generating highly consistent and complex sequences over minutes (Kanervisto et al., 2025), we achieve substantial model speed-up (79x) with a modest quality trade-off. Additionally, we demonstrate that this model can be trained to reproduce high-quality, consistent gameplay using a dataset containing approximately one day (28 hours) of gameplay data. Our results demonstrate progress towards removing barriers to the use of world models in settings where real-time inference and data efficiency are essential.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Manufacturing Differential Sparsity: Planner-Hardware Co-Design for Efficient World Model Planning》归入 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

JEPA-style latent world models let embodied agents plan toward new goals with model predictive control, but they are still too slow for real-life embodied control: sampling-based MPC repeats predictor inference tens of thousands of times per plan, and even on high-end GPUs one planning step can take tens of seconds. We observe substantial latent locality in this repeated-inference workload: the predictor maps close actions to close latents, and inside a quantized predictor this closeness takes a form that hardware can exploit, differential bit-level sparsity. Unlike an input stream, whose temporal similarity is given, a planner itself decides which candidates it evaluates together, so the planner can choose its own references and, with reference forest sampling, manufacture more sparsity: it couples the sampled action sequences of each group to a shared root, with competitive planning quality. We build the accelerator on the insights of the rich line of bit-level sparsity accelerators and design it and the planner tightly coupled as a single co-design: the planner increases sparsity and exposes structure for hardware scheduling, and the bit-exact hardware effectively uses the sparsity that the planner creates. On three JEPA world models, our co-design is 1.28-1.80x faster than a dense accelerator and uses 38-52% less energy, and it is 1.22-1.58x faster than prior bit-sparse design and uses 16-36% less energy, with the same area. To our knowledge, our planner is the first planner that manufactures differential bit-level sparsity across its candidates and exposes its structure for hardware scheduling, and our co-design is the first algorithm-hardware co-design for JEPA-style world-model planning.

</details>

《Inertial Video World Models》归入 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：InertialWorld, Real2Sim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

The physical world has inertia: it evolves from what already exists rather than being recreated at every instant. However, many existing video world models lack an explicit mechanism for inertial state evolution, leaving the continuity of motion and structure implicit in video prediction. During autoregressive rollouts, repeated reconstruction can accumulate errors and destabilize the predicted dynamics. To address this limitation, we introduce InertialWorld, an Inertial Video World Model that makes inertia a structural prior for autoregressive prediction: existing scene evidence is carried across time rather than reconstructed at every step. Central to this formulation is Inertial Memory, which turns memory from passive predictive context into an evolving video state with two complementary views: a dense Eulerian grid preserves scene-aligned evidence, while persistent Lagrangian carriers bind local content to learned canonical coordinates. At each step, the model transports the carrier content according to action-conditioned motion, predicts the complete next video state from the transported carriers and dense grid, and uses a learned gate to combine transported and newly predicted content into the memory for the next step. Extensive experiments across multiple domains, including robot manipulation, 3D game simulation, and open-world navigation, demonstrate that InertialWorld achieves competitive autoregressive prediction performance against strong video world model baselines. Codes link: https://github.com/YuanGao-YG/InertialWorld

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Perception into Imagination: Executable Memory for World Models》归入 世界模型、强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MiniWorld, PlaNet, SearchWorld, TT-MBRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Physical agents must use what they previously saw or touched after visibility or contact is lost. Their actions change the implications of these remembered facts. We introduce executable perceptual memory (EPM), a world-model framework that advances explicit perceptual state alongside neural latent dynamics during imagination. Its evolving read-out informs reward prediction, value estimation and policy learning. We align reward inputs with the transitions that produced them and supervise the reward head on open-loop rollouts of recorded actions. On Memory Maze $13\times13$, EPM reaches 30.37 return at 1M interactions, compared with 23.37 when the same evolving read-out informs only policy and value on a common perceptual front end: a gain of 7.00 [3.80, 10.21]. The complete method also improves control on $9\times9$ and in MiniWorld. Probes of remembered, out-of-view arrivals show stronger reward anticipation in both visual implementations. Concentration, tactile recovery and real tactile frames demonstrate complementary roles of remembered facts in imagined-policy training, motion-dependent decisions and sensory-content retention. EPM connects persistent perception to the predicted consequences and learning signals of a world model for Physical AI.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models have shown great potential in serving as a simulator for robot manipulation. However, existing models are either fit to a single environment or lack ability to simulate long-horizon contact-rich events. We present DreamFAR, a world model whose dynamics model is trained from scratch on a large robot-native teleoperation dataset, with no internet-video pretraining. We present an architecture and learning recipe designed for training on robot-native data. DreamFAR is significantly smaller than the high-performance video generation models, yet shows better understanding of contact and causality, and runs 10 FPS on a commercial GPU. Experimentally, DreamFAR significantly outperforms existing models in predicting the long-horizon future. We further demonstrate various applications where DreamFAR can be used, including policy evaluation and mixed-data co-training. Project page: https://www.yimingdou.com/DreamFAR/

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present WM-Quant, to our knowledge, the first post-training quantization (PTQ) framework designed specifically for JEPA-style world models. Although world models typically feature relatively few parameters, action planning algorithms often require thousands of inferences, which can quickly become computationally expensive. PTQ is a natural way to reduce this cost. However, we show that in world models using autoregressive model predictive control (MPC) rollouts, quantization error can compound with each predictor step, resulting in action rankings from the quantized model becoming near-random. WM-Quant addresses this with three components that build on existing PTQ methods: early-encoder precision, control-path preservation, and rollout-aware scaling. Early-encoder precision protects accurate latent state and goal information to mitigate long-term error propagation. Control-path preservation allows the world model predictor to distinguish between fine-grained actions, significantly improving action rankings and execution. Rollout-aware scaling jointly calibrates encoder and per-step predictor activation ranges with respect to an entire rollout rather than a single prediction, reducing the cumulative rollout error. Across multiple world models, environments, and PTQ methods, WM-Quant substantially improves planning success at low bit-widths, approaching full-precision performance at W4A4.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Every_Camera_Grows_Up_Camera-Height_Regression_in_Interactive_Video_World_Models|Every Camera Grows Up: Camera-Height Regression in Interactive Video World Models]]

![[assets/2611.21782_figure.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《Every Camera Grows Up: Camera-Height Regression in Interactive Video World Models》归入 世界模型、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Habitat-Sim, LingBot-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Interactive video world models serve as simulators for embodied agents: from a starting image and an action trajectory, they generate the views the agent would see. The starting image fixes how high the camera is, for example 0.15 m for a floor robot or 2.2 m for an overhead camera, and a faithful simulator must keep this height as the camera moves. We show that six recent open-weight models fail to do so: as motion replaces the starting view with generated content, they move the camera toward human eye level, a failure we call camera-height regression. Our benchmark, EyeHeight, pairs six rendered elevations (0.15 m to 2.2 m) and four calibrated real-world rig heights with scale-free multi-view height estimation. All six models lose their initial height during a $180^\circ$ turn-and-walk rollout: initialized at 0.15 m, only 0-20% of clips end within $1.3\times$ of the starting height as inferred viewpoints converge to human heights of 1.1 m to 1.5 m, whereas ground-truth renders remain within a few percent. In Matrix-Game models, homography-tracked floor motion stays consistent with constant elevation during rotation even as newly outpainted room geometry yields a higher reconstructed viewpoint. Regression grows with rotation angle, begins while the start frame remains in context, and largely reverses in SANA-WM when a $360^\circ$ turn restores the initial view. We trace this pull to human-centric video priors in which floor-level views are nearly absent: in SANA-WM, textual height cues and dense floor-disparity maps do not prevent it, whereas classifier-free text guidance during the first half of denoising strengthens it. Combining unguided sampling with height-balanced fine-tuning recovers more retention but costs 5 MUSIQ points, a loss fixed during those same early steps. Viewpoint drift alone shifts the paths predicted by visual navigation policies.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Right Ranking, Wrong Scale: Reproducing the Robotic World Model's Dynamics Model and Testing the Uncertainty of Its Follow-up》归入 世界模型、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We reimplement the dynamics model of the Robotic World Model (RWM) and test the uncertainty estimate that its follow-up, RWM-U, uses to penalize reward in offline policy optimization. Our implementation matches the released model's gradients. On held-out data, training on the model's own rollouts beats one-step training at the preregistered long horizon for all ten episodes, but loses on short one-step windows. RWM-U's ensemble disagreement ranks rollout errors better than forecast step (Spearman rho = 0.90 at 100 steps), yet its spread substantially understates error: one-sigma coverage on the released checkpoint is 4.6% at 100 steps, versus 68% for a calibrated Gaussian. Rolling ensemble members on their own predictions raises coverage to 33%. The implemented squared-error loss on sampled predictions drives each member's variance toward zero. A horizon-specific rescaling restores one-sigma coverage under leave-one-episode-out evaluation, but not two-sigma coverage. We also identify two defects in the released evaluation pipeline. The study covers one robot, one gait and one dataset; it trains no policy.

</details>

- **作者**: Almog Anschel, Sarah Keren
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 世界模型, 大模型, 具身智能, 强化学习

- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Agent, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《WoRD: Diffusion-Based Posterior Inference for Online Goal Recognition》归入 机器人、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HPNet, QCNet, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Goal recognition is the task of inferring an agent's intended goal from its observed behavior. Most existing work focuses on high-level symbolic recognition, abstracting away the continuous motion through which agents reach their goal. Methods that do reason over continuous trajectories rely on an explicit dynamics model that is hard to acquire and rarely transfers across settings. Neither approach explicitly learns how agents move toward each candidate goal, limiting accuracy on ambiguous trajectory prefixes where such knowledge is most needed. We present WoRD (Waypoint Recognition with Diffusion), which uses a learned trajectory prior to infer the agent's goal from partial observations. At each step, WoRD runs one warm-started denoising chain per candidate goal, steers each chain toward the observed trajectory through a closed-form log-likelihood gradient, and reweights the resulting samples by Bayes' rule into a calibrated posterior over goals. We evaluate WoRD on simulated robot settings, pedestrian trajectories, and vehicle trajectories using four metrics targeting distinct failure modes. Across all three settings, the same inference procedure preserves calibrated multimodal beliefs through ambiguous prefixes where filter- and classifier-based observers collapse to a single mode or grow overconfident.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Capability Composition in Heterogeneous Teams via Barrier-Guided Diffusion Sampling》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

The agents in a heterogeneous team can cooperate to accomplish tasks that exceed their individual capabilities. The team trajectories for a cooperative task can be generated by a generative model, provided that the model has been trained on demonstrations of the agents cooperating. Yet, demonstrations of cooperation are costly and may not exist for tasks that the team has never performed. The conditions under which the agents can combine their capabilities, however, are physical properties of the team and are known before any demonstration is collected. We encode the conditions as constraints on the team trajectories, and we enforce the constraints while sampling from generative models trained on demonstrations of each agent alone. We prove that the proposed method can generate every team trajectory that satisfies the constraints, although the demonstrations contain no cooperation. Hence the constraints determine which team trajectories can be generated, and the demonstrations determine how likely each team trajectory is. Numerical results on two cooperative tasks show the effectiveness of the method, which generates cooperative team trajectories that satisfy the constraints and requires no retraining when agents join or leave the team. Finally, we implement the method on a multi-robot testbed.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Pressure Drop: Gravity Vector Estimation from a Distributed Barometric Pressure Sensor Array》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

While gravity is often used in mobile robotics as a global constraint for Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) algorithms, most estimation methods rely on indirect observation of the gravity vector. In this paper, we propose the use of altimeter measurements to directly observe the direction of the gravity vector. We provide a theoretical evaluation of the estimation noise and validate it through extensive experimental data, along with an evaluation of the impact of intersensor distance on our gravity vector estimates. Experimental results demonstrate that a median error of $0.87^\circ$ on the tilt estimation can be achieved with our system in semi-static scenarios. Furthermore, we demonstrate the potential of this approach by comparing it with state-of-the-art methods based on Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs). We highlight that we achieve similar performance to that of IMU-based systems in highly dynamic scenarios and even outperform in specific cases of accelerometer measurement saturation. Finally, an additional experiment shows the potential use within a robotic system through the integration into an Iterative Closest Point (ICP) algorithm.

</details>

![[assets/2611.23118_figure.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《Auditing a Learned Simulator for Legged Robots: The Robotic World Model on ANYmal D》归入 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A legged robot can learn to walk inside a learned simulator: a neural network that predicts the robot's next state and is run forward on its own predictions. They are usually trained and checked one step ahead, although a policy uses them for a hundred steps or more. We audit one published system end to end, without a GPU: the dynamics model of the Robotic World Model (RWM) for the ANYmal D quadruped, and the uncertainty estimate of its follow-up, RWM-U. We rebuilt the model to match the released code's outputs, losses and gradients exactly, measured 101 trained simulators, 200 compressed copies and 38 simulators of three other architectures, and trained policies inside simulators that we tested in a hand-built MuJoCo model of the robot. Training on the model's own rollouts beat one-step training at 368 steps on all ten held-out episodes (registered), although one-step training was better one step ahead. One-step checks therefore misjudge design choices: pruning 30% of the weights kept 90% of the model's skill one step ahead and 39% at 2 s. Ensemble disagreement ranked rollout error well (Spearman +0.90 at 100 steps), yet covered 4.6% of outcomes at one sigma, against 68% for a calibrated estimate, as each network's own variance collapses under the implemented loss. Two checks that need no recorded future ranked some pools of simulators well, but neither was shown better than 8-step error in a primary test. At the robot level, accuracy has not predicted walking: all 18 fine-tuned copies we tested predicted better than the released model one and eight steps ahead, yet 13 gave failing policies, and registered tests of whether any check ranks simulators by their policies were not settled. In a secondary test, running the authors' policy inside each simulator ranked them well. We also report nine of our own claims that we withdrew. Scope: one robot, one gait, simulated data, no hardware.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Sim2CBF: From Forward Simulations to Implicit Control Barrier Function Safety Filtering without Model Derivatives》归入 机器人、具身智能、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Failure avoidance in complex robot-environment interactions is challenging because the underlying dynamics are difficult to model accurately, and available predictive models may not provide reliable derivatives. We present Sim2CBF, a failure-avoidance framework that solves the Control Barrier Function (CBF)-Safety Filter (SF) optimization problem using only forward-model evaluations, thereby avoiding differentiation of the predictive model. Specifically, we approximate the CBF constraint using finite differences and local linear interpolation over sampled control inputs while preserving failure-avoidance guarantees. The underlying CBF is implicit and constructed from backup-policy rollouts, enabling the use of expressive predictive models such as physics-based simulators and learned world models. Compared with purely sampling-based SFs, our formulation requires fewer control samples and yields less conservative behavior than direct nominal-backup blending. We evaluate Sim2CBF in simulation, hardware box-toppling, and vision-based control tasks, demonstrating its applicability to contact-rich and learned dynamics, as well as its adaptability to changes in the predictive model.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Arboreus: A Realistic Boreal Forest Simulator with Topographic Terrain and Satellite-Informed Parametric Tree Distributions》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AirSim, GazeboSim, IsaacSim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This work presents Arboreus, a realistic Boreal forest simulator, developed to support statistical validation of robotic autonomy algorithms in Boreal forest environments. Arboreus presents an automated methodology for synthesizing large-scale, data-informed, plausible forest worlds from parametric trees, height-variant terrain, and satellite-informed tree density distributions. The parametric generation of dominant Boreal tree species such as Birch, Spruce, and Pine is guided by trunk, canopy, and foliage parameters, extracted from growth studies in the Nordic areas. Tree distribution in an area of interest is guided by the Copernicus satellite dataset, which provides tree cover density and dominant species type at up to 10m raster resolution. The forest floor generation is guided by topographic data from the Swedish cadastral agency to produce realistic data-informed terrains. Given coordinates and extents of forested areas of interest, Arboreus synthesizes data-informed plausible forests, while offering flexibility to inject user observations of real forests (detail, age, dimensionality and tree count), which is critical for realistic synthetic data generation and for the testing of feature-sensitive autonomy algorithms. The applicability of Arboreus for benchmarking and validation of robot autonomy for different robot mobilities is demonstrated in Gazebosim, Unreal Engine, and Isaac Sim physics engines. The applicability of Arboreus as a source of synthetic forest data and its potential to support downstream forest resource mapping research (volume estimation, tree instance segmentation and stem attribute estimation) is also demonstrated. Arboreus has been open-sourced along with a dataset comprising 25 Boreal forest worlds of size 1000m2 corresponding to three real forest sites.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FlowVLA, HUGE-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Natural language-guided UAV navigation enables intuitive human-robot interaction and flexible autonomous flight in complex environments. However, reliable navigation requires accurate instruction following, continuous task progress tracking, and safe obstacle avoidance, which remain challenging for existing world-action models that primarily focus on action prediction. To address these limitations, we propose a world-action learning framework that integrates three complementary mechanisms: a language-conditioned action interface with targeted post-training for instruction-responsive motion, recurrent instruction-consumption prediction to track task progress and determine termination, and Gaussian-scene post-training to enhance obstacle avoidance through trajectory imitation and differentiable geometric feedback. Closed-loop evaluations and ablation studies demonstrate improvements in sequential task completion, safe goal reaching, and timely termination. Furthermore, direct one-step denoising reduces inference overhead, and real-world indoor and outdoor flights validate the framework's effectiveness.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This paper presents the design and implementation of Auriga Robotics' mobile manipulator robot, developed at the Robotics and Intelligent Automation Laboratory, Department of Electrical Engineering, Shahid Beheshti University. Hereafter, this platform is referred to as the @Work Robot. The robot targets industrial tasks, with an emphasis on improving efficiency in repetitive or hazardous work environments. It uses a 4-wheel Mecanum drive for omnidirectional movement and a 5-degree-of-freedom manipulator arm with a 3D-printed gripper for object handling and navigation. The electronics consist of custom PCB boards built around ESP32 microcontrollers, together with an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX that handles high-level processing and real-time system control. On the software side, Hector SLAM provides mapping and localization, A* handles path planning, and YOLO performs real-time object detection. These modules draw on fused sensor data to deliver robust navigation and reliable collision avoidance. Overall, the system shows effective hardware-software integration, with consistent performance in autonomous navigation, object handling, and task execution in industrial-like settings.

</details>

![[assets/2611.22773_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22773)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22773
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/Guarded_Swarms_in_the_Loop_A_ROS_2_PX4_Digital_Twin_for_Hybrid_Digital-Analog_Coordination_of_UAV_UGV_Swarms|Guarded Swarms in the Loop: A ROS 2/PX4 Digital Twin for Hybrid Digital-Analog Coordination of UAV/UGV Swarms]]
- **作者**: Uwe M. Borghoff, Paolo Bottoni, Luca D'Aurizio, Remo Pareschi
- **cs 子类**: cs.CR
