#### 研究背景与动机

《Guarded Swarms in the Loop: A ROS 2/PX4 Digital Twin for Hybrid Digital-Analog Coordination of UAV/UGV Swarms》归入 机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Cryptography and Security 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Swarms of unmanned aerial and ground vehicles (UAV/UGV) operating in contested environments cannot rely on software alone for physical safety, since the software stack and its networks are part of the attack surface. The Guarded Swarms architecture addresses this by pairing a digital coordination layer, built on Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Nets (TB-CSPN), with an independent analog safety layer that can veto unsafe actuation commands. So far, the architecture has been specified and argued for, but not executed. We present the first executable instantiation of Guarded Swarms as a digital twin built on ROS 2, the PX4 autopilot in software-in-the-loop mode, and Gazebo, with two UAVs and one UGV in a coastal counter-UAS scenario. The TB-CSPN transition chain, from multi-modal sensing through human authorization to vehicle tasking, is mapped one-to-one onto ROS 2 nodes, and the analog layer is modeled as a per-vehicle Virtual Safety Board that gates every command. Eight groups of experiments show that the three negative guarantees of the architecture (no mission from conflicting, temporally incoherent, or unauthorized evidence) hold at three distinct stages of the chain in all 60 counterfactual runs; that threat confirmation is bounded by the narrowest sensing modality; that the safety layer exercises its full modeled envelope independently of the coordination layer; and, under fault injection, that it prevents every collision caused by a forged mission or a compromised worker once its veto is enforced by an independent gate, whereas a veto left to the compromised worker prevents none. The twin also exposes implementation-level hazards that the architectural specification does not address, and it draws an explicit line between the properties that simulation can validate and those that only hardware can.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Diffusion models are increasingly adopted in robotics and control due to their capability to express multiple feasible solutions to a given task. However, the role of stochasticity in the diffusion model-sampler across denoising remains poorly understood. We introduce a per-timestep counterfactual diagnostic that tests how changing the sampled noise affects the final output. Across five model-sampler configurations spanning three robotics benchmarks, diverse architectures, domains, and conditioning mechanisms, we reveal highly varied temporal sensitivity profiles, where noise-induced deviations can amplify, partially contract, or undergo near-total attenuation downstream. From these profiles, we define stochastic diversity windows (SDWs): compact intervals that identify where sampler stochasticity has the strongest measured influence on the final output. Guided by these windows, we apply two heuristic interventions: targeted stochastic amplification, which increases sample diversity exploration of the solution space, and an SDW-hybrid sampler, which replaces DDPM steps with DDIM skips in low-impact regions, resulting in network evaluation reductions of up to 40-60% and wall-clock time by up to 85%, with no consistent changes detected in feasibility, cost, or diversity across held-out tasks.

</details>

《Body Schema Policy: Body as the Vision-Action Interface for Efficient Robot Loco-Manipulation》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AVA-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Currently, robots can learn whole-body loco-manipulation through scale: the strongest policies have billions of parameters and still need task-specific fine-tuning. Humans have limited processing capacity, yet learn and act efficiently through a body schema that organizes what they see and how they move. We introduce Body Schema Policy (BSP), an imitation learning policy that builds such a schema into the vision-action interface. Its latent body schema places joint and link tokens on the robot's kinematic tree, where learned screw motions carry features between links. Through body-queried vision, each body part retrieves its own visual evidence, which conditions the denoising of its actions. With this inductive bias, BSP returns an action chunk faster than any policy we compare. Compact, body-specific visual readouts also emerge without spatial supervision: manipulation commands depend strongly on task objects, while locomotion commands respond more broadly to the scene. On nine simulated Unitree G1 whole-body tasks, BSP matches the $Ψ_0$ humanoid foundation model with $75\times$ fewer parameters and nearly doubles Diffusion Policy's success. The same holds on a real G1 and a Fetch mobile manipulator.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/VLM-guided_Grounding_Expert_for_Generalised_Referring_Expression_Comprehension|VLM-guided Grounding Expert for Generalised Referring Expression Comprehension]]

![[assets/2611.22625_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22625](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22625)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22625
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/VLM-guided_Grounding_Expert_for_Generalised_Referring_Expression_Comprehension|VLM-guided Grounding Expert for Generalised Referring Expression Comprehension]]
- **作者**: Mingcheng Zhu, Wentian Xu, Tao Lin, Shiying Duan, Ziyun Liang, Xuecheng Qi, Junyang Chen, Kuo Li, Zhifan Wan, Dong Wang, Richard Wang, Yang Gao
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.8（加权：具身智能 0.6，大模型 0.5，机器人 0.7）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《VLM-guided Grounding Expert for Generalised Referring Expression Comprehension》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：YOLO-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Generalised referring expression comprehension (GREC) aims to identify and localise the zero, one or multiple objects referred to by a language expression. In robotic manipulation, grounding also needs to remain accurate when requests for the same objects are paraphrased or embedded in additional context in robotics. However, grounding expert models are sensitive to instruction variation, while generative vision--language models (VLMs) struggle with complete target recovery and rejection of absent targets. These complementary limitations motivate combining the contextual representations of VLMs with the direct spatial prediction capabilities of grounding experts. We therefore introduce VLM-guided Grounding Expert (VGE), which combines a frozen VLM with a trainable grounding expert through layerwise joint attention and text-conditioned instance scoring. These interfaces leverage multimodal understanding of the VLM to guide object-query refinement and candidate selection while retaining direct bounding-box prediction. Across three datasets, VGE achieves the highest averaged F1 score among the evaluated methods on all five test splits, with differences of at most 1.2 percentage points across instruction conditions within each split. On the dataset with robotic scenes, VGE also achieves the highest F1 across instruction variants and no-target, single-target and multiple-target cases. VGE provides a $5.3$--$13.0\times$ inference speedup over the baseline with the same VLM base model. These results support combining VLM context with specialised spatial prediction to address both grounding challenges, with particularly strong performance in robotic scenes. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/Task_Convergent_Flow_Matching_for_Generative_Robotics|Task Convergent Flow Matching for Generative Robotics]]

![[assets/2611.22609_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22609](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22609)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22609
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/Task_Convergent_Flow_Matching_for_Generative_Robotics|Task Convergent Flow Matching for Generative Robotics]]
- **作者**: Lorenzo Paiola, Maximilian Stölzle, Giorgio Grioli, Daniela Rus, Cosimo Della Santina
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 1.4（加权：具身智能 0.3，机器人 1.1）

#### 研究背景与动机

《Task Convergent Flow Matching for Generative Robotics》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Typical-Set。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Successful manipulation requires the manipulated objects to progress toward a desired task outcome. Generative policies can produce plausible robot motions without ensuring this progress, allowing successive action chunks to stall or undo earlier changes to the scene. We introduce a task-centric inference-time refinement framework for flow matching policies that evaluates candidate action chunks through parallel physics-based rollouts and searches for motions satisfying sufficient conditions for task convergence. Task-level performance funnels bound temporary regressions while requiring net progress over each chunk. We establish sufficient conditions under which repeated funnel satisfaction yields convergence throughout execution, and use interval-wise Lipschitz bounds to verify continuous-time funnel satisfaction from sampled rollouts. To accommodate contact-rich hybrid dynamics, we refine the flow's source seeds using derivative-free Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES), constrained to radial quantile bands of the source distribution to help retain the learned motion prior. This formulation supports both Euclidean and pose-valued Riemannian Flow Matching (RMFM) policies. On PushT and three-cube stacking, refinement raises success from 31.3% to 88.5% and from 77.0% to 86.0%. On PushT, whose coverage score responds continuously to the motion of the block, a learned funnel lowers the average maximum loss of achieved progress by 19% relative to constraining the endpoint of each chunk alone, at a similar success rate. With the radial band, 11.3% of the executed motion windows are out of distribution, a rate within the range of held-out demonstrations, compared with 34.1% for an unconstrained search.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/From_Attack_Tree_to_Model_Checker_Binding_CAPEC_Patterns_to_Verify_Robotic_System_Security|From Attack Tree to Model Checker: Binding CAPEC Patterns to Verify Robotic System Security]]

![[assets/2611.22408_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22408)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22408

- **作者**: Juel Hussain, Louise A. Dennis, Clare Dixon, Marie Farrell

#### 研究背景与动机

《From Attack Tree to Model Checker: Binding CAPEC Patterns to Verify Robotic System Security》归入 机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Cryptography and Security 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Cryptography and Security 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：可见文本中未给出明确实验数字或完整对比表。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Security-minded verification of autonomous and robotic systems presupposes an attacker, yet the path from a threat catalogue to a checkable attacker model is still traversed by hand. This research preview presents the theory and construction of a pipeline that mechanises it. Each leaf of an attack tree is mapped to a CAPEC attack pattern and written in first-order logic as a rule: the attacker's prerequisites imply the attack's effect. Casting the pattern as such a rule is what makes it operational - its variables can be bound to a specific system's parts, and its prerequisites can then be tested against that system under a chosen configuration of security controls. Binding is our means of verifying whether a system is vulnerable to a given attack: from a bound pattern we derive an attacker model, turn its effect into a temporal-logic property, and check that property against the system with and without a countermeasure, reporting the control that defeats the attack. We describe the signature, schemas, profiles for approach, and explore how CAPEC can support reasoning about robotics security.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/ROSMonitoring_in_Action_Runtime_Monitoring_and_Enforcement_for_ROS_2_Actions|ROSMonitoring in Action: Runtime Monitoring and Enforcement for ROS 2 Actions]]

![[assets/2611.22407_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22407](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22407)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22407
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/ROSMonitoring_in_Action_Runtime_Monitoring_and_Enforcement_for_ROS_2_Actions|ROSMonitoring in Action: Runtime Monitoring and Enforcement for ROS 2 Actions]]
- **作者**: Giacomo Paltrinieri, Angelo Ferrando

- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《ROSMonitoring in Action: Runtime Monitoring and Enforcement for ROS 2 Actions》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robotic applications often rely on long-running operations, such as navigation, manipulation, and inspection, which in ROS~2 are commonly implemented as actions. Unlike topics and services, actions involve a structured lifecycle comprising goal submission and acceptance, feedback, status updates, result retrieval, and cancellation. Existing versions of ROSMonitoring support topics and services, but do not reconstruct this lifecycle or expose action-level events to a runtime-verification oracle. In this paper, we extend ROSMonitoring with runtime monitoring and enforcement for ROS~2 actions. Actions are declared directly in the monitoring configuration and automatically instrumented through the underlying ROS~2 communication endpoints. The generated monitor reconstructs action-level events, correlates them through goal identifiers, maintains the state of active goals, and translates violations into protocol-aware enforcement operations. These include goal rejection, cancellation, message suppression, and protocol-compliant responses generated directly by the monitor when the original communication cannot be forwarded. We evaluate the implementation through controlled stress tests that vary the number of action invocations, workload size, and clientâserver topology. The results show approximately linear growth in monitoring cost with repeated action invocations under a fixed workload, while larger action workloads and a greater number of configured clientâserver pairs introduce additional overhead. These results characterise the scaling behaviour of the current implementation under the tested sequential workloads while highlighting the main performance limitations of the current synchronous implementation.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This paper presents BehaVerify, a verification toolbox for behavior trees (BTs) and Neuro-Symbolic BTs. BTs are a graphical language for planning and sequencing behaviors - similar to extended state machines - and are increasingly used in safety-critical domains. BehaVerify enables users to specify a BT using a Domain Specific Language (DSL) with formal syntax and semantics. This language further includes Linear Temporal Logic and Computation Tree Logic specifications. BehaVerify uses an efficient encoding into nuXmv, called fastforwarding, to quickly verify if a specification holds over the BT. Additionally, BehaVerify can generate implementations of the BT in Python and Haskell. Through Python, BehaVerify has been integrated with PyTrees and Robot Operating System (ROS) and has been deployed to several autonomous robotic systems, including for verifying runtime monitors. While prior works have developed the underlying theory, this manuscript focuses on the toolbox usage, functionality and implementation. BehaVerify is open source: https://github.com/verivital/behaverify

</details>

![[assets/2611.22399_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22399](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22399)

- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/Plenty_to_FRET_About_From_Hazard_Analysis_to_Formal_Requirements_for_Robot-Assisted_Dressing|Plenty to FRET About: From Hazard Analysis to Formal Requirements for Robot-Assisted Dressing]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《Plenty to FRET About: From Hazard Analysis to Formal Requirements for Robot-Assisted Dressing》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Human-Computer Interaction 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Understanding requirements for systems involving humans and robots is essential for providing robust evidence that such systems behave correctly. Requirements formalisation approaches such as the Formal Requirements Elicitation Tool (FRET) can support the transition from unstructured natural language to formal representations suitable for verification. In this paper, we formalise 146 hazard-derived natural-language requirements for a Robot-Assisted Dressing (RAD) case study, previously classified using a taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork (HRT) requirements. Through an iterative process involving domain-expert clarification, these were represented by 117 unique FRETISH requirements. Formalisation exposed ambiguities, temporal and probabilistic assumptions, and cases where source requirements needed to be decomposed or could be consolidated because they expressed the same formal behaviour. Temporal analysis showed that 70.1% of the unique FRETISH requirements use either immediate-response or ordering constraints (immediately or before), while formalisation also exposed relationships between requirements across HRT taxonomy categories. We further analyse the formal semantics and realisability of the resulting requirements. The results show that HRT classification and formalisation provide complementary views: the taxonomy captures the teamwork concern represented by a requirement, while FRETISH makes the required behaviour explicit. We report practical lessons and limitations of applying FRET to HRT requirements and identify directions for further work.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A large amount of high-level Petri net formalisms has been investigated and deployed in academic settings over the past decades, but these deployments fail to reach practical applications, despite the strong guarantees they can bring to stakeholders. In particular, the lack of direct benefits from end-users reduces the appeal of Petri nets, both due to the overly simple classical Petri net formalism, and the overly specialized high-level formalisms, on top of state-space explosion issues. In this work, we investigate how a middle-ground could be reached, by separating the implementation-specific aspects of software or hardware behaviors, from the classical Petri net semantics. The goal is to benefit from the sought-after aspects of Petri nets, namely their simple firing semantic and model-checking capabilities, while still being able to augment their behavior to best reflect the modelled system. We present a formal model of Interpreted Petri nets with data-aware transitions and tokens. We illustrate this formalism by modelling the executive layer of a robotic system, and provide a lightweight tool to augment classical Petri nets for general purpose applications, as well as data-aware state-space exploration for model-checking. Their specialization in the context of autonomous robotics is showcased, with a complete industrial inspection mission use-case, to compare simulation and model-checking results between the classical and the new data-aware Petri net semantic.

</details>

---

![[assets/2611.22388_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22388](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22388)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22388
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Hidden_Variables_Behind_Real-Robot_Data_Quality_Lessons_from_Hundreds_of_Thousands_of_Hours|Hidden Variables Behind Real-Robot Data Quality: Lessons from Hundreds of Thousands of Hours]]
- **作者**: Maniformer Real-World Manipulation Team, Che Tsang Calvin, Xingyu Chen, Bowen Jiang, Naixiang Gao, Xiongfeng Cai, Enlin Gu, Ruiying Li, Peize Li, Mianrui Zhu, Haomiao Zhang, Xin He...
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 1.4（加权：具身智能 0.3，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《Hidden Variables Behind Real-Robot Data Quality: Lessons from Hundreds of Thousands of Hours》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

As real-robot data collection scales, collection-system design shapes both operator efficiency and the behaviors learned by robot policies. Workspace limitations, control-interface friction, and latency can introduce pauses, detours, and corrective motions into otherwise successful demonstrations. We investigate these effects across embodiment, teleoperation control, and data consistency, combining a kinematic study of 25 dual-arm robots, controlled teleoperation experiments, and real-robot evaluation of fine-tuned $π_{0.5}$ policies. The embodiment study shows that positional reachability overstates orientation-aware task coverage and that modest shoulder motion can substantially expand the effective workspace. Teleoperation experiments show that parallelizing body-part control reduces task completion time by 43-53%, while compliant control improves success and reduces peak contact forces on constrained and contact-rich tasks. Policy-learning experiments show that demonstrations must capture the visual information underlying action choices: active-search demonstrations improve arm-selection accuracy from chance (50%) to 100%. Calibration experiments reveal that mixed-calibration training data remain usable in our tested configurations when an inference-aligned subset is retained, whereas inference-time miscalibration causes all 30 trials to fail. Policies also retain approximately 90% of the extra path length in detour-contaminated demonstrations, showing how inefficient operator motions persist in learned behavior. Together, these findings connect collection-system design to demonstration quality and downstream policy execution. They motivate evaluating effective workspace, control efficiency, observation completeness, calibration alignment, and motion quality before scaling collection, and addressing system-induced compensations at their source.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/FailureGen_Scalable_Robot_Failure_Data_Generation_with_World_Models|FailureGen: Scalable Robot Failure Data Generation with World Models]]

![[assets/2611.22367_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22367](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22367)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22367

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《FailureGen: Scalable Robot Failure Data Generation with World Models》归入 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Ctrl-World, MiraBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot failures are difficult to collect at scale, limiting the range of failure scenarios represented in robot learning datasets. We present FailureGen, a method for generating synthetic videos with temporal event annotations by reusing action trajectories from recorded failures. The method comprises four stages: (1) fine-tuning an action-conditioned Cosmos world model on successful and failed real executions; (2) annotating source recordings to identify failure-associated donor segments and compatible successful hosts; (3) transferring donor motion into host contexts, with optional spatial offsets, to generate continuous video rollouts; and (4) annotating and filtering the generated samples according to observed events and visual quality. We study two implementations with different tasks and robot configurations, with visual failure detection serving as one downstream application. In a preliminary comparison, a detector trained on real and generated data achieves an average precision of 0.51, compared with 0.41 using real data alone, and detects 33 of 36 failure intervals, compared with 28 of 36. By reusing observed failures in additional contexts, FailureGen expands the data available for studying robot failures and developing downstream learning methods.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Where_Do_Human-to-Robot_Transfer_Gains_Come_From|Where Do Human-to-Robot Transfer Gains Come From?]]

![[assets/2611.22302_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22302](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22302)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22302
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Where_Do_Human-to-Robot_Transfer_Gains_Come_From|Where Do Human-to-Robot Transfer Gains Come From?]]

- **归属领域**: 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《Where Do Human-to-Robot Transfer Gains Come From?》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ImageNet, Meta-World, MetaWorld, ResNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Human data can support robot policy pretraining, but gains attributed to a data recipe may also reflect subset size, training variability, or downstream adaptation. In an HPT implementation on a 20-task MetaWorld suite with three training blocks, a constrained random 5,000-recording subset matches most of the task-informed subset's gain over the 40,000-recording pool at 20,000 updates (3.69 of 4.29 percentage points), leaving equal-size selection differences of 0.60 points at 5,000 recordings and 0.45 points at 1,250 recordings, with wide paired intervals. Across 12 comparisons, task orderings repeat across seeds for several contrasts, but a fixed predictor built from the other two seeds trails the mean or difficulty reference in at least one held-out fold for every comparison. Neither mechanism probe shows its predicted positive interaction: task-slot mismatch yields an interaction of $-0.0347$ points after adaptation, and in a six-task observation experiment the observation-by-pretraining interaction changes sign between seeds ($-18.29$ and $+1.04$ points) while full-state pretraining gains are positive in both ($+6.37$ and $+2.31$ points). This retrospective study shows how equal-size and capacity controls, seed-level reporting, and targeted interventions change the interpretation of apparent transfer gains.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/CoSphere-SLAM_Multi-Agent_Panoramic_Gaussian_Splatting_SLAM_with_Spherical_Loop_Closure|CoSphere-SLAM: Multi-Agent Panoramic Gaussian Splatting SLAM with Spherical Loop Closure]]

![[assets/2611.22235_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22235](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22235)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22235
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/CoSphere-SLAM_Multi-Agent_Panoramic_Gaussian_Splatting_SLAM_with_Spherical_Loop_Closure|CoSphere-SLAM: Multi-Agent Panoramic Gaussian Splatting SLAM with Spherical Loop Closure]]
- **作者**: Qi Shao, Zhihao Cao, Shuhao Zhai, Taoyu Wu, Zhuoxiao Li, Anh Nguyen, Long Hu, Xianzhi Li, Hong Liang, Baoru Huang, Liang Li
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《CoSphere-SLAM: Multi-Agent Panoramic Gaussian Splatting SLAM with Spherical Loop Closure》归入 机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Dense collaborative mapping is essential for search and rescue, building inspection, and warehouse automation, where panoramic cameras capture the entire surroundings in a single frame. Unlike pinhole agents that share views only at similar headings, panoramic agents observe overlapping scenes at any 3D orientation, yet searching full rotations also produces mutually consistent false loop closures across distinct symmetric rooms. Moreover, continuous monocular scale drift splits rigidly merged maps at revisited surfaces, while depth noise at short baselines distorts loop translation directions. To address these challenges, we propose CoSphere-SLAM, the first multi-agent Gaussian splatting SLAM framework for panoramic cameras, whose spherical loop closure aligns spherical features over $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ via fast Fourier correlation across arbitrary orientations and rejects symmetric false closures through global $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ consensus. Building on these closures, directional global optimization solves a per-keyframe $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ pose graph with log-scale regularization to correct scale drift while separating optical-flow translation directions from depth-based magnitudes. Finally, omnidirectional Gaussian fusion transforms each Gaussian with its keyframe similarity and renders Gaussians at their maximum ray response to jointly refine poses and maps. Experiments on indoor and cross-session multi-agent panoramic benchmarks demonstrate that CoSphere-SLAM achieves state-of-the-art trajectory accuracy and rendering quality among single-agent and multi-agent methods. Project website: https://cosphere-slam.github.io.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《TRIDENT: Efficient Triple-Task Learning of Dehazing, Depth, and Uncertainty Estimation for Underwater 3-D Robot Visual Perception》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Underwater visual systems often suffer from blurry textures and low color contrast due to the inevitable light propagation. These issues can significantly degrade the perception of stable robotic operations. In this paper, we introduce a novel learning-based sensing system that tackles the multidimensional vision tasks in underwater; concretely, we deal with image enhancement, depth estimation, and uncertainty for 3-D visual systems. Also, we propose a TRIDENT model in a fast and lightweight manner; TRIDENT consists of three parallelized decoders and one backbone structure for efficient feature sharing. In addition, it is designed to be trained to express complex parameterization. In experimental evaluation on several standard datasets, we demonstrate that TRIDENT significantly outperforms other existing methods on image enhancement and depth estimation. Despite performing three tasks, our model has better efficiency than the others for both memory size and inference time. Finally, our joint learning approach demonstrates robustness in feature matching and seamlessly extends from 2-D to 3-D vision tasks. Supplements are available at https://sites.google.com/view/underwater-trident/home

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

HandUMI captures robot-free bimanual demonstrations with a hand-worn parallel-jaw interface. Swappable fingertips match the robot's installed gripper; Quest/PICO controllers track hands and encoders measure aperture. Robot-agnostic recordings are retargeted by inverse kinematics to seven simulated bimanual arms (median position error $\leq 1.2$ mm; $\leq 4.7$% of episodes rejected). On bimanual towel folding, 562 robot-free demonstrations train policies on real PiPER arms: ACT succeeds in 12/20 attempts and fine-tuned $π_{0.5}$ in 7/20; ACT on the first 101 and base $π_{0.5}$ each succeed in 0/20. Hardware and software are open source (Apache-2.0), and a pair costs USD 557 with Quest 3S, excluding the optional ZED Mini and host computer. Project website: https://murobotics.ai/research/handumi.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《RoboTeam: Benchmarking Vision-Based Multi-Agent Control of Many Robot Arms in Real Time》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CLiMBench, EmbodiedBench, ReflexBench, RoCoBench, RobotWorld, VIKI-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multimodal agents can now control robot arms by calling tools and writing code. Existing benchmarks fall short in three ways: most test one or two arms on short tasks, many pause the world while the agent thinks, and many give it ground-truth object positions. We present RoboTeam, a simulated benchmark where agents control 4 to 16 robot arms in real time from camera images alone. Its 36 tasks are long jobs that the arms must do together, such as passing timbers or building a picture from hundreds of cubes. We start from the most flexible agent system, the adaptive system, where one agent decides when to fork delegates. With it, gpt-6.1-sol and opus-5.5 complete 28 and 27 tasks, and four other models at most 3. But at a high price: a typical task takes gpt-6.1-sol 3.5 hours and 218 million input tokens, and a third of its steps fail at least once and must be retried. We then compare it with three fixed systems: single-agent, hierarchical, and decentralized. All four complete a similar number of tasks but differ in time and tokens. The hierarchical and decentralized systems are faster but use up to 1.9 times the tokens, and the single agent uses fewer tokens but takes 1.6 times as long, so the adaptive system lies between them. Even so, no model or system is yet fast, cheap, and reliable at once. RoboTeam measures these limits where earlier benchmarks cannot: long multi-arm jobs, camera images only, and a world that never pauses.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/DailyBrick_Language-Guided_Synthesis_of_Brick_Assembly_Instructions|DailyBrick: Language-Guided Synthesis of Brick Assembly Instructions]]

![[assets/2611.22132_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22132](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22132)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22132
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/DailyBrick_Language-Guided_Synthesis_of_Brick_Assembly_Instructions|DailyBrick: Language-Guided Synthesis of Brick Assembly Instructions]]
- **作者**: Hang Zhang, Qijian Tian, Yuan Xie, Xuhong Wang, Xin Tan
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 大模型, 具身智能

- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《DailyBrick: Language-Guided Synthesis of Brick Assembly Instructions》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：BrickNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Generating a LEGO model from a user request is more than a plausible 3D shape generation. LEGO models require physical support per brick, and each brick should be placed unblocked. Current methods address this issue by training an expert large language model. We reduce the problem of generating extremely long context on all brick connections to that of writing a program that builds LEGO models like a human, considering each intermediate step could be executed. We introduce DailyBrick , a multi-stage agentic system that produces a LEGO model together with a brick-by-brick assembly instruction for human building and robot assembly. We design a multi-stage harness with roles to help LLMs generate more effectively. A versioned construction SDK compiles multi-level decisions, from semantic subparts to individual bricks, into canonical operations. An external Meta-Reviewer agent replays and inspects the generated package, verifies the model and its construction process, identifies failures, traces them to the responsible stage, and routes them to bounded repairs or to editable candidates for later skill or tool updates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DailyBrick generates LEGO models based on simple user requests better than recent agentic systems, and show its utility in downstream applications such as robot manipulations on model assembly from instructions and brick sets (https://SpatialAgent.github.io/DailyBrick/).

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Low-speed Limit Cycle Walking of Planar X-shaped Bipedal Robot with Special Properties》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This paper proposes a novel planar semicircular-footed X-shaped bipedal robot in which the center of mass of each leg link is at the same position as the hip joint, and discusses a method for generating a stable limit cycle gait on level ground. First, we outline the assumptions of the robot model with four degrees of freedom, and develop the mathematical equations of motion and collision. Second, we set the relative hip-joint angle as a control output and design a control system to follow it to the desired trajectory specified as a fifth-order function of time. Third, we mathematically show that there are special properties: the angular velocity of the swing leg does not change before and after the collision, and the time-integral of the control input becomes zero in a steady gait. We also mathematically show that the model has the feature that approximate linearization is effective and the antigravity effect of semicircular feet can be utilized. Through numerical simulations, we verify the validity of the theoretical results, and analyze some fundamental gait properties such as convergence speed and mechanical energy restoration.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/ReLAP_Latent_Action_Pretraining_through_Recurrent_Video_Prediction_in_JEPA_Space|ReLAP: Latent Action Pretraining through Recurrent Video Prediction in JEPA Space]]

![[assets/2611.22077_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.22077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22077)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22077
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/ReLAP_Latent_Action_Pretraining_through_Recurrent_Video_Prediction_in_JEPA_Space|ReLAP: Latent Action Pretraining through Recurrent Video Prediction in JEPA Space]]
- **作者**: R. Gnana Praveen, Ali Rouzbayani, Zhiying Jiang, Bidhan Roy
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 世界模型, 大模型, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《ReLAP: Latent Action Pretraining through Recurrent Video Prediction in JEPA Space》归入 机器人、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CoLA-World, RotVLA, UniVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Videos provide a rich source of physical interaction for learning robot behavior without action annotations, yet effective latent actions must capture not only local transitions but also how those transitions evolve over time. We introduce ReLAP, a latent-action pretraining framework that couples local transition learning with recurrent multi-step prediction in a frozen JEPA representation space. A shared inverse dynamics model infers compact continuous latent actions from adjacent observations, while a latent world model recurrently predicts the future JEPA features. Because each predicted feature becomes the context for the next transition, errors at later steps backpropagate through the rollout to earlier latent actions, encouraging temporally coherent dynamics beyond pairwise reconstruction. We transfer this representation to a vision-language model using two complementary objectives: latent distillation from the frozen inverse teacher and recurrent future-feature prediction through the frozen latent world model. The resulting policy is then adapted to robot demonstrations with action flow matching. At deployment, the explicit latent-action and world-model branches are removed, and the fine-tuned VLM predicts physical actions directly, reducing inference complexity. With only 11M pretraining samples and 1.06B trainable parameters, ReLAP achieves 97.9% on standard LIBERO and 70.0% over zero-shot LIBERO-Plus perturbations. On the LARY latent-action benchmark, ReLAP ranks second in both mean regression error and mean classification accuracy among the compared methods. These results demonstrate that recurrent action-free latent action pretraining can learn compact, temporally structured, and transferable representations for downstream robot control.

</details>

![[assets/2611.21938_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Javad Amirian, Armin Kiani, Loïc Schaeffer, Tim Sprecher, Mohamed Taha, Karthik Yadati
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《HubEgo-100K: In-the-Wild Egocentric Video of Skilled Work for Robot Learning》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot policies trained by imitation generalize poorly outside the environments and objects seen in training. Published data-scaling studies find that generalization grows with the diversity of training environments and objects rather than with more demonstrations per environment. Robot data is costly to collect across many environments, whereas egocentric human video can be recorded in the wild at scale; existing egocentric video, however, mostly shows daily-life activity and contains little skilled work in real workplaces. We present HubEgo-100K, a dataset built for diversity. This version of the paper covers the first release, v1.0: 10,124 hours of 1080p egocentric video with synchronized 100 Hz inertial (IMU) data from one head-mounted phone camera. In v1.0, 12,227 contributors record their own unscripted work in 15,548 environments (each one contributor's own setting of one type, such as their kitchen or workshop) across 11 setting types, 30 work categories, 56 task types and 35 action families, with 83% of hours in workplace settings. The release comprises 72,754 episodes with machine-generated action and language annotations, in LeRobot and MCAP formats under CC BY 4.0. We measure diversity along each axis, show that inside v1.0, with episodes in random order, new contributors and words keep appearing, and relate the measurements to published evidence on six data properties tied to policy performance, including hand visibility and inertial sensing. We also document collection, curation, privacy measures and data format, and provide a datasheet. Later versions of this paper will add benchmarks and cover further releases toward 100,000 hours.

</details>

- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 世界模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《ASTRA: ASV Simulator for Transferable Realistic Autonomy》归入 机器人、具身智能、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ASVSim, AirSim, MarineGym。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Autonomy development for marine autonomous surface vehicles (ASVs) remains challenging because field testing is costly and difficult to reproduce. Existing marine simulation tools provide mature capabilities in vessel dynamics, robotics integration, and visual rendering, but these strengths are emphasized differently across platforms. This paper presents ASTRA, an ASV simulation framework built in NVIDIA Isaac Sim that combines marine vehicle dynamics, photorealistic perception, and Robot Operating System (ROS 2) autonomy interfaces within a common environment. ASTRA incorporates a Fossen-style marine dynamics model together with geometry-based buoyancy and programmable spectral wave disturbances, enabling non-planar hydrostatic and wave-induced motion. The simulator reproduces the sensor configuration of a physical BlueBoat testbed, including GPS, IMU, LiDAR, and RGB-D sensing exposed through ROS 2. This allows localization, perception, planning, and control software to be developed against interfaces consistent with the physical platform. The BlueBoat's existing ROS 2 localization and perception modules were ported directly into ASTRA, while targeted zero-propulsion experiments were used to verify the implemented wave-body coupling. The simulated roll response was consistent with the expected non-oscillatory hydrostatic behavior, and heave remained highly coherent with the generated wave field, with approximately unity gain at the dominant wave frequency in the tested sea states. ASTRA is intended as an open-source testbed for marine autonomy development, simulation-based benchmarking, and sim-to-real experimentation.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Monitoring oil spills with fleets of Autonomous Surface Vehicles (ASVs) requires mapping a heterogeneous pollutant field from sparse, noisy measurements under tight time and energy budgets. Informative Path Planning (IPP) addresses this problem by coupling a reconstruction model, which infers the field and its uncertainty, with a planner that decides where to sample next. Prior work has focused on planning strategies and has largely relied on Gaussian Processes (GPs), leaving the calibration of the model's uncertainty underexplored. We study how uncertainty calibration conditions multi-vehicle IPP with a fleet of ASVs that coordinates online through a shared uncertainty map. We introduce Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) as an uncertainty-calibrated information model. We benchmark EDL, Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensembles against a GP in closed loop with five planners on physics-based oil spill simulations. Across 60 maps, the choice of model dominates the outcome, explaining 80\% of the variance of the final reconstruction error, against 1\% for the planner among the deep models. EDL achieves the lowest error and the highest IoU under every planner, is the best calibrated model and runs at 3.9 ms per step. With 50 cells per vehicle it reconstructs the spill better than MC Dropout and Deep Ensembles with 150. Deep Ensembles become more confident as their error grows, whereas EDL is unaffected. Robustness of the information model to the data its own planner collects is therefore a requirement of uncertainty-aware IPP.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《An Open-Source da Vinci X/Xi Instrument Module for Laparoscopic Surgery Research》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Studying electrosurgical tasks on a research robot requires control of both instrument motion and energy activation, along with a record of when each action occurs. This paper presents an open-source module for da Vinci X/Xi instruments on a UR5/UR5e robotic arm. Five motors transmit torque through timing belts to spring-loaded drive discs. An electrosurgical interposer accepts software commands, gives the surgeon's foot pedals priority, and records the source of each activation request. In bench testing, the interposer recorded all 12 activation and deactivation events from six pedal presses. Ex vivo demonstrations included monopolar and bipolar coagulation of splenic tissue and monopolar cutting during gallbladder dissection. Optical tracking of the Fenestrated Bipolar Forceps showed that calibration brought pitch and yaw gains from 0.904 and 1.14 to 1.00 and 1.02, respectively. The module enables researchers to record instrument motion, video, and the timing and source of electrosurgical commands during laparoscopic tasks.

</details>
